   99: HYBRID_MODELS = {"hybrid_xgboost", "hybrid_lightgbm", "hybrid_catboost"}
  100: BOOSTING_MODELS = {"xgboost_pure", "lightgbm_pure"}
  101: 
  102: MODEL_NAMES = {
  103:     "ridge": "Ridge",
  104:     "bayesian_ridge": "Bayesian Ridge",
  105:     "ard": "ARD",
  106:     "xgboost_pure": "XGBoost",
  107:     "lightgbm_pure": "LightGBM",
  108:     "catboost_pure": "CatBoost",
  109:     "hybrid_xgboost": "Hybrid XGBoost",
  110:     "hybrid_lightgbm": "Hybrid LightGBM",
  111:     "hybrid_catboost": "Hybrid CatBoost",
  112: }
  113: 
  114: MODEL_TYPES = {
  115:     "ridge": "linear",
  116:     "bayesian_ridge": "linear",
  117:     "ard": "linear",
  118:     "xgboost_pure": "boosting",
  119:     "lightgbm_pure": "boosting",
  120:     "catboost_pure": "boosting",
  180: # =============================================================================
  181: 
  182: def _get_model_params(model_id: str, horizon_config: dict) -> Optional[dict]:
  183:     """Get model-specific params. Linear->None, CatBoost->translated."""
  184:     if model_id in LINEAR_MODELS:
  185:         return None
  186: 
  187:     if model_id in CATBOOST_MODELS:
  188:         return {
  189:             "iterations": horizon_config.get("n_estimators", 50),
  190:             "depth": horizon_config.get("max_depth", 3),
  191:             "learning_rate": horizon_config.get("learning_rate", 0.05),
  192:             "l2_leaf_reg": horizon_config.get("reg_alpha", 0.5),
  193:             "verbose": False,
  194:             "allow_writing_files": False,
  195:         }
  196: 
  197:     if model_id in HYBRID_MODELS:
  198:         if "catboost" in model_id:
  199:             return {
  200:                 "iterations": horizon_config.get("n_estimators", 50),
  201:                 "depth": horizon_config.get("max_depth", 3),
  202:                 "learning_rate": horizon_config.get("learning_rate", 0.05),
  203:                 "verbose": False,
  204:                 "allow_writing_files": False,
  205:             }
  206:         return horizon_config
  207: 
  208:     return horizon_config
  209: 
  210: 
  211: # =============================================================================
  212: # WALK-FORWARD VALIDATION
  213: # =============================================================================
  214: 
  215: def walk_forward_validate(
  216:     X: np.ndarray,
  217:     y: np.ndarray,
  218:     model_id: str,
  219:     params: Optional[dict],
  220:     horizon: int,
  221:     n_folds: int = 5,
  222:     annualization_days: int = 252,
  223: ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
  224:     """Walk-forward validation returning full metrics + predicted/actual arrays."""
  225:     n = len(X)
  226:     initial_train = int(n * 0.6)
  227:     step = (n - initial_train) // n_folds
  228: 
  229:     all_preds = []
  230:     all_actuals = []
  231:     from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler
  232: 
  233:     for fold in range(n_folds):
  234:         train_end = initial_train + fold * step
  235:         test_start = train_end
  236:         test_end = min(test_start + step, n)
  237: 
  238:         if test_end <= test_start or train_end < 50:
  239:             continue
  240: 
  241:         model = ModelFactory.create(model_id, params=params, horizon=horizon)
  242:         # Purge the trailing training observations whose forward label overlaps
  243:         # the test block. Without this embargo, H20-H30 labels leak future test
  244:         # returns into the fitted sample and directional accuracy is overstated.
  245:         purged_train_end = max(0, train_end - horizon)
  246:         x_train = X[:purged_train_end]
  247:         x_test = X[test_start:test_end]
  248:         # Fit preprocessing strictly inside each fold.  Scaling on the complete frame
  249:         # leaks test-fold distribution statistics into training and inflates DA.
  250:         if model.requires_scaling:
  251:             fold_scaler = StandardScaler().fit(x_train)
  252:             x_train = fold_scaler.transform(x_train)
  253:             x_test = fold_scaler.transform(x_test)
  254:         if purged_train_end < 50:
  255:             continue
  256:         model.fit(x_train, y[:purged_train_end])
  257:         preds = model.predict(x_test)
  258:         actuals = y[test_start:test_end]
  259: 
  260:         all_preds.extend(preds.tolist())
  261:         all_actuals.extend(actuals.tolist())
  262: 
  263:     if not all_preds:
  264:         return {
  265:             "da": 0.5, "rmse": 0.0, "mae": 0.0, "r2": 0.0,
  266:             "sharpe": 0.0, "pf": 1.0, "max_drawdown": 0.0, "total_return": 0.0,
  267:             "predicted_returns": [], "actual_returns": [],
  268:         }
  269: 
  270:     preds_arr = np.array(all_preds)
  271:     actuals_arr = np.array(all_actuals)
  272: 
  273:     correct = np.sum(np.sign(preds_arr) == np.sign(actuals_arr))
  274:     da = correct / len(actuals_arr)
  275: 
  276:     # Confidence diagnostics: absolute predicted return is the model's natural
  277:     # confidence proxy. These are audit metrics, not a post-hoc promotion rule;
  278:     # thresholds must be selected on training data in a future deployment gate.
  279:     confidence = np.abs(preds_arr)
  280:     selective = {}
  281:     for fraction, suffix in ((0.50, "top50"), (0.25, "top25")):
  282:         k = max(1, int(np.ceil(len(confidence) * fraction)))
  283:         idx = np.argsort(confidence)[-k:]
  284:         selective[f"selective_da_{suffix}"] = float(
  285:             np.mean(np.sign(preds_arr[idx]) == np.sign(actuals_arr[idx]))
  390:         state = "risk_off"
  391:     elif z < -1.5 and mr > mf:
  392:         state = "risk_on"
  393:     else:
  394:         state = "transition"
  395:     return state, float(z), 1
  396: 
  397: 
  398: # =============================================================================
  399: # TRAIN MODELS + GENERATE DATA FOR ONE WEEK
  400: # =============================================================================
  401: 
  402: def generate_week_data(
  403:     df_full: pd.DataFrame,
  404:     cutoff_date: pd.Timestamp,
  405:     week_label: str,
  406:     is_latest_week: bool,
  407:     feature_cols: List[str],
  408:     annualization_days: int = 252,
  409: ) -> Tuple[List[Dict], Dict[str, Dict[int, float]], Dict, float, pd.Timestamp]:
  410:     """
  411:     Train 9 models x 7 horizons for one week, run walk-forward.
  412: 
  413:     Returns:
  414:         csv_rows, raw_predictions, wf_results, base_price, inference_date
  415:     """
  416:     from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler
  417: 
  418:     inference_year = cutoff_date.year
  419:     df = df_full[df_full["date"] <= cutoff_date].copy()
  420:     feature_cols = list(feature_cols)
  421:     feature_mask = df[feature_cols].notna().all(axis=1)
  422:     df_clean = df[feature_mask].reset_index(drop=True)
  423: 
  424:     if len(df_clean) < 100:
  425:         raise ValueError(f"Not enough data for {week_label}: {len(df_clean)} rows")
  426: 
  427:     X = df_clean[feature_cols].values.astype(np.float64)
  428:     base_price = float(df_clean["close"].iloc[-1])
  429:     inference_date = df_clean["date"].iloc[-1]
  430: 
  431:     X_latest_raw = X[-1:].copy()
  432:     regime_shift_score = compute_regime_shift_score(X)
  433:     regime_action = "RETRAIN_REQUIRED" if regime_shift_score >= 1.5 else "NORMAL"
  434:     eligible_for_signal = regime_action == "NORMAL"
  435:     direction_regime, direction_shift_z, direction_regime_persistence = compute_direction_regime(df_clean["close"])
  436: 
  437:     logger.info(f"  {week_label}: {len(df_clean)} rows, base={base_price:.2f}, inf_date={inference_date.date()}")
  438: 
  439:     now_str = datetime.now().isoformat()
  440:     inf_date_str = str(inference_date.date())
  441:     week_suffix = week_label.replace("-", "_")
  442: 
  443:     # Collect results
  444:     all_metrics: Dict[str, Dict[int, Dict]] = {}
  445:     raw_predictions: Dict[str, Dict[int, float]] = {}
  446:     wf_results: Dict[str, Dict[int, Dict]] = {}
  447: 
  448:     for model_id in MODEL_IDS:
  449:         all_metrics[model_id] = {}
  450:         raw_predictions[model_id] = {}
  451:         wf_results[model_id] = {}
  452: 
  453:         for horizon in HORIZONS:
  454:             try:
  455:                 future_price = df_clean["close"].shift(-horizon)
  456:                 y = np.log(future_price / df_clean["close"]).values
  457:                 valid_mask = ~np.isnan(y)
  458:                 y_valid = y[valid_mask]
  459: 
  460:                 if len(y_valid) < 50:
  461:                     continue
  462: 
  463:                 h_config = get_horizon_config(horizon)
  464:                 params = _get_model_params(model_id, h_config)
  465: 
  466:                 model_tmp = ModelFactory.create(model_id, params=params, horizon=horizon)
  467:                 use_scaled = model_tmp.requires_scaling
  468:                 # Keep raw features here; walk_forward_validate owns fold-local scaling.
  469:                 X_wf = X[valid_mask]
  470: 
  471:                 wf = walk_forward_validate(
  472:                     X_wf, y_valid, model_id, params, horizon,
  473:                     annualization_days=annualization_days,
  474:                 )
  475:                 wf_results[model_id][horizon] = wf
  476:                 all_metrics[model_id][horizon] = {
  477:                     "da": wf["da"], "rmse": wf["rmse"], "mae": wf["mae"], "r2": wf["r2"],
  478:                     "selective_da_top50": wf.get("selective_da_top50", 0.0),
  479:                     "selective_coverage_top50": wf.get("selective_coverage_top50", 0.0),
  480:                     "selective_da_top25": wf.get("selective_da_top25", 0.0),
  481:                     "selective_coverage_top25": wf.get("selective_coverage_top25", 0.0),
  482:                     "sharpe": wf["sharpe"], "pf": wf["pf"],
  483:                     "max_drawdown": wf["max_drawdown"], "total_return": wf["total_return"],
  484:                 }
  485: 
  486:                 model = ModelFactory.create(model_id, params=params, horizon=horizon)
  487:                 if use_scaled:
  488:                     final_scaler = StandardScaler().fit(X[valid_mask])
  489:                     model.fit(final_scaler.transform(X[valid_mask]), y_valid)
  490:                     pred_return = float(model.predict(final_scaler.transform(X_latest_raw))[0])
  491:                 else:
  492:                     model.fit(X[valid_mask], y_valid)
  493:                     pred_return = float(model.predict(X_latest_raw)[0])
  494: 
  495:                 raw_predictions[model_id][horizon] = pred_return
  496: 
  497:             except Exception as e:
  498:                 logger.warning(f"    {model_id} H={horizon}: {e}")
  499: 
  500:     # Model averages
  501:     model_avg_da = {}
  502:     model_avg_rmse = {}
  503:     for model_id in MODEL_IDS:
  504:         das = [m["da"] for m in all_metrics[model_id].values()]
  505:         rmses = [m["rmse"] for m in all_metrics[model_id].values()]
  506:         model_avg_da[model_id] = float(np.mean(das)) if das else 0.0
  507:         model_avg_rmse[model_id] = float(np.mean(rmses)) if rmses else 0.0
  508: 
  509:     best_overall = max(model_avg_da, key=model_avg_da.get) if model_avg_da else None
  510: 
  511:     best_per_horizon: Dict[int, Tuple[str, float]] = {}
  512:     for horizon in HORIZONS:
  513:         best_da, best_model = -1.0, None
  514:         for model_id in MODEL_IDS:
  515:             if horizon in all_metrics[model_id]:
  516:                 da = all_metrics[model_id][horizon]["da"]
  517:                 if da > best_da:
  518:                     best_da, best_model = da, model_id
  519:         if best_model is not None:
  520:             best_per_horizon[horizon] = (best_model, best_da)
  520:             best_per_horizon[horizon] = (best_model, best_da)
  521: 
  522:     # Build CSV rows
  523:     csv_rows = []
  524:     for model_id in MODEL_IDS:
  525:         for horizon in HORIZONS:
  526:             if horizon not in all_metrics[model_id]:
  527:                 continue
  528: 
  529:             m = all_metrics[model_id][horizon]
  530:             h_cat = HORIZON_CATEGORIES.get(horizon, "short")
  531:             is_best = model_id == best_overall
  532:             is_best_h = best_per_horizon.get(horizon, (None, 0))[0] == model_id
  533:             best_da_h = best_per_horizon.get(horizon, (None, 0))[1]
  534: 
  535:             # Forward forecast row (all weeks)
  536:             csv_rows.append({
  537:                 "record_id": f"FF_{model_id}_h{horizon}_{week_suffix}",
  538:                 "view_type": "forward_forecast",
  539:                 "model_id": model_id,
  540:                 "model_name": MODEL_NAMES.get(model_id, model_id),
  541:                 "model_type": MODEL_TYPES.get(model_id, "unknown"),
  542:                 "horizon_days": horizon,
  543:                 "horizon_label": f"H={horizon}",
  544:                 "horizon_category": h_cat,
  545:                 "inference_week": week_label,
  546:                 "inference_year": inference_year,
  547:                 "inference_date": inf_date_str,
  548:                 "direction_accuracy": m["da"],
  549:                 "regime_shift_score": regime_shift_score,
  550:                 "regime_action": regime_action,
  551:                 "eligible_for_signal": eligible_for_signal,
  552:                 "direction_regime": direction_regime,
  553:                 "direction_shift_z": direction_shift_z,
  554:                 "direction_regime_persistence": direction_regime_persistence,
  555:                 "selective_da_top50": m["selective_da_top50"],
  556:                 "selective_coverage_top50": m["selective_coverage_top50"],
  557:                 "selective_da_top25": m["selective_da_top25"],
  558:                 "selective_coverage_top25": m["selective_coverage_top25"],
  559:                 "rmse": 0.0,
  560:                 "mae": 0.0,
  561:                 "r2": 0.0,
  562:                 "sharpe": m["sharpe"],
  563:                 "profit_factor": m["pf"],
  564:                 "max_drawdown": m["max_drawdown"],
  565:                 "total_return": m["total_return"],
  566:                 "wf_direction_accuracy": m["da"],
  567:                 "model_avg_direction_accuracy": model_avg_da[model_id],
  568:                 "model_avg_rmse": model_avg_rmse[model_id],
  569:                 "is_best_overall_model": is_best,
  570:                 "is_best_for_this_horizon": is_best_h,
  571:                 "best_da_for_this_horizon": best_da_h,
  572:                 "image_path": f"forward_{model_id}_{week_suffix}.png",
  573:                 "image_backtest": f"backtest_{model_id}_h{horizon}.png",
  574:                 "generated_at": now_str,
  575:                 "image_forecast": f"forward_{model_id}_{week_suffix}.png",
  576:             })
  577: 
  578:             # Persist backtest metrics for every generated week.  Plots remain latest-week-only,
  579:             # but hiding earlier folds made a multi-week OOS audit impossible.
  580:             # Persist every week's walk-forward result for OOS stability audits;
  581:             # forward plots remain limited to the latest week below.
  582:             if week_label:
  583:                 csv_rows.append({
  584:                     "record_id": f"BT_{model_id}_h{horizon}_{week_suffix}",
  585:                     "view_type": "backtest",
  586:                     "model_id": model_id,
  587:                     "model_name": MODEL_NAMES.get(model_id, model_id),
  588:                     "model_type": MODEL_TYPES.get(model_id, "unknown"),
  589:                     "horizon_days": horizon,
  590:                     "horizon_label": f"H={horizon}",
  591:                     "horizon_category": h_cat,
  592:                     "inference_week": week_label,
  593:                     "inference_year": inference_year,
  594:                     "inference_date": inf_date_str,
  595:                     "direction_accuracy": m["da"],
  596:                     "regime_shift_score": regime_shift_score,
  597:                     "regime_action": regime_action,
  598:                     "eligible_for_signal": eligible_for_signal,
  599:                     "direction_regime": direction_regime,
  600:                     "direction_shift_z": direction_shift_z,
  601:                     "direction_regime_persistence": direction_regime_persistence,
  602:                     "selective_da_top50": m["selective_da_top50"],
  603:                     "selective_coverage_top50": m["selective_coverage_top50"],
  604:                     "selective_da_top25": m["selective_da_top25"],
  605:                     "selective_coverage_top25": m["selective_coverage_top25"],
  606:                     "rmse": m["rmse"],
  607:                     "mae": m["mae"],
  608:                     "r2": m["r2"],
  609:                     "sharpe": m["sharpe"],
  610:                     "profit_factor": m["pf"],
  611:                     "max_drawdown": m["max_drawdown"],
  612:                     "total_return": m["total_return"],
  613:                     "wf_direction_accuracy": m["da"],
  614:                     "model_avg_direction_accuracy": model_avg_da[model_id],
  615:                     "model_avg_rmse": model_avg_rmse[model_id],
  616:                     "is_best_overall_model": is_best,

  631: 
  632: def _setup_dark_fig(figsize=(12, 6)):
  633:     fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=figsize, facecolor=S["bg"])
  634:     ax.set_facecolor(S["bg"])
  635:     ax.tick_params(colors=S["text"], labelsize=9)
  636:     for spine in ["top", "right"]:
  637:         ax.spines[spine].set_visible(False)
  638:     for spine in ["left", "bottom"]:
  639:         ax.spines[spine].set_color(S["grid"])
  640:     ax.grid(True, alpha=0.15, color=S["grid"])
  641:     return fig, ax
  642: 
  643: 
  644: def _style_ax(ax):
  645:     """Apply dark style to an axis."""
  646:     ax.set_facecolor(S["bg"])
  647:     ax.tick_params(colors=S["text"], labelsize=8)
  648:     for spine in ["top", "right"]:
  649:         ax.spines[spine].set_visible(False)
  650:     for spine in ["left", "bottom"]:
  651:         ax.spines[spine].set_color(S["grid"])
  652:     ax.grid(True, alpha=0.15, color=S["grid"])

  654: 
  655: def generate_backtest_image(wf_result: Dict, model_id: str, horizon: int, output_dir: Path):
  656:     """Generate backtest PNG: scatter + equity curve (latest week only)."""
  657:     preds = np.array(wf_result["predicted_returns"])
  658:     actuals = np.array(wf_result["actual_returns"])
  659:     if len(preds) < 5:
  660:         return
  661: 
  662:     fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(14, 5), facecolor=S["bg"])
  663: 
  664:     # Left: Predicted vs Actual scatter
  665:     ax = axes[0]
  666:     _style_ax(ax)
  667:     correct = np.sign(preds) == np.sign(actuals)
  668:     ax.scatter(actuals[correct], preds[correct], c=S["green"], s=18, alpha=0.7, label="Correct", zorder=3)
  669:     ax.scatter(actuals[~correct], preds[~correct], c=S["red"], s=18, alpha=0.7, label="Wrong", zorder=3)
  670:     lims = [min(actuals.min(), preds.min()), max(actuals.max(), preds.max())]
  671:     ax.plot(lims, lims, "--", color=S["accent"], alpha=0.5, lw=1)
  672:     ax.axhline(0, color="#64748b", lw=0.5, alpha=0.5)
  673:     ax.axvline(0, color="#64748b", lw=0.5, alpha=0.5)
  674:     ax.set_title(f"Predicted vs Actual (DA={wf_result['da']*100:.1f}%)",
  675:                  color=S["text"], fontsize=11, fontweight="bold")
  676:     ax.set_xlabel("Actual Return", color=S["text"], fontsize=9)
  677:     ax.set_ylabel("Predicted Return", color=S["text"], fontsize=9)
  678:     ax.legend(fontsize=8, loc="upper left", facecolor=S["bg"],
  679:               edgecolor=S["grid"], labelcolor=S["text"])
  680: 
  681:     # Right: Equity curve
  682:     ax2 = axes[1]
  683:     _style_ax(ax2)
  684:     signals = np.sign(preds)
  685:     trade_rets = signals * actuals
  686:     equity = np.cumprod(1 + trade_rets) * 100
  687:     bh_equity = np.cumprod(1 + actuals) * 100
  688:     ax2.plot(equity, color=S["accent"], lw=1.5, label="Strategy")
  689:     ax2.plot(bh_equity, color=S["blue"], lw=1, alpha=0.5, label="Buy & Hold")
  690:     ax2.axhline(100, color="#64748b", lw=0.5, ls="--", alpha=0.5)
  691:     ax2.set_title(
  692:         f"Equity (Return={wf_result['total_return']*100:.1f}%, Sharpe={wf_result['sharpe']:.2f})",
  693:         color=S["text"], fontsize=11, fontweight="bold")
  694:     ax2.set_xlabel("Trade #", color=S["text"], fontsize=9)
  695:     ax2.set_ylabel("Equity (base=100)", color=S["text"], fontsize=9)
  696:     ax2.legend(fontsize=8, loc="upper left", facecolor=S["bg"],
  697:                edgecolor=S["grid"], labelcolor=S["text"])
  698: 
  699:     model_name = MODEL_NAMES.get(model_id, model_id)
  700:     fig.suptitle(f"{model_name} â€” H={horizon} Day Backtest",

  163: # =============================================================================
  164: # DATA LOADING (mirrors L5a DAG pattern)
  165: # =============================================================================
  166: 
  167: def load_full_dataset(cfg: ForecastingSSOTConfig) -> pd.DataFrame:
  168:     """Load full daily OHLCV + macro dataset, build SSOT features for `cfg`'s asset.
  169: 
  170:     Uses shared ForecastingDatasetLoader (DB-first with parquet fallback).
  171:     USD/COP: 4 macro columns (DXY, WTI, VIX, EMBI). BTC: DXY + VIX only.
  172:     """
  173:     loader = ForecastingDatasetLoader(cfg, project_root=PROJECT_ROOT)
  174:     df, _ = loader.load_dataset()
  175:     return df
  176: 
  177: 
  178: # =============================================================================
  179: # MODEL PARAMS ROUTING (same as L5a DAG)
  180: # =============================================================================
  270:     preds_arr = np.array(all_preds)
  271:     actuals_arr = np.array(all_actuals)
  272: 
  273:     correct = np.sum(np.sign(preds_arr) == np.sign(actuals_arr))
  274:     da = correct / len(actuals_arr)
  275: 
  276:     # Confidence diagnostics: absolute predicted return is the model's natural
  277:     # confidence proxy. These are audit metrics, not a post-hoc promotion rule;
  278:     # thresholds must be selected on training data in a future deployment gate.
  279:     confidence = np.abs(preds_arr)
  280:     selective = {}
  281:     for fraction, suffix in ((0.50, "top50"), (0.25, "top25")):
  282:         k = max(1, int(np.ceil(len(confidence) * fraction)))
  283:         idx = np.argsort(confidence)[-k:]
  284:         selective[f"selective_da_{suffix}"] = float(
  285:             np.mean(np.sign(preds_arr[idx]) == np.sign(actuals_arr[idx]))
  286:         )
  287:         selective[f"selective_coverage_{suffix}"] = float(k / len(confidence))
  288: 
  289:     rmse = float(np.sqrt(np.mean((preds_arr - actuals_arr) ** 2)))
  290:     mae = float(np.mean(np.abs(preds_arr - actuals_arr)))
  291:     ss_res = np.sum((actuals_arr - preds_arr) ** 2)
  292:     ss_tot = np.sum((actuals_arr - np.mean(actuals_arr)) ** 2)
  293:     r2 = float(1 - ss_res / ss_tot) if ss_tot > 0 else 0.0
  294: 
  295:     signals = np.sign(preds_arr)
  296:     trade_returns = signals * actuals_arr
  297: 
  298:     periods_per_year = annualization_days / max(horizon, 1)
  299:     mean_ret = np.mean(trade_returns)
  300:     std_ret = np.std(trade_returns)
  301:     sharpe = float(mean_ret / std_ret * np.sqrt(periods_per_year)) if std_ret > 0 else 0.0
  302: 
  303:     gains = trade_returns[trade_returns > 0].sum()
  304:     losses = abs(trade_returns[trade_returns < 0].sum())
  305:     pf = float(gains / losses) if losses > 0 else (2.0 if gains > 0 else 1.0)
  306: 
  307:     equity = np.cumprod(1 + trade_returns)
  308:     total_return = float(equity[-1] - 1) if len(equity) > 0 else 0.0
  309:     running_max = np.maximum.accumulate(equity)
  310:     drawdown = (equity - running_max) / running_max
  311:     max_drawdown = float(abs(drawdown.min()))
  312: 
  313:     return {
  314:         "da": da, "rmse": rmse, "mae": mae, "r2": r2,
  315:         "sharpe": sharpe, "pf": pf, "max_drawdown": max_drawdown,
  316:         "total_return": total_return,
  317:         "predicted_returns": all_preds, "actual_returns": all_actuals,
  318:         **selective,
  319:     }
  320: 
  321: 
  322: # =============================================================================
  323: # WEEK DISCOVERY
  324: # =============================================================================
  325: 
  326: def find_last_n_weeks(df: pd.DataFrame, n: int = 7) -> List[Tuple[str, pd.Timestamp]]:
  327:     """Find the last n weeks in the dataset, returning (week_label, last_trading_day)."""
  328:     dates = df["date"].sort_values().unique()
  329: 
  330:     # Group by ISO week, keep last trading day per week
  331:     weeks = {}
  332:     for d in dates:
  333:         ts = pd.Timestamp(d)
  334:         year, week_num, _ = ts.isocalendar()
  335:         week_key = f"{year}-W{week_num:02d}"
  336:         weeks[week_key] = ts
  337: 
  338:     sorted_weeks = sorted(weeks.items(), key=lambda x: x[1])
  339:     return sorted_weeks[-n:]

  341: 
  342: def get_target_dates_and_actuals(
  343:     df_full: pd.DataFrame,
  344:     inference_date: pd.Timestamp,
  345:     base_price: float,
  346: ) -> Dict[int, Dict]:
  347:     """For each horizon, find the target trading date and actual price (if available)."""
  348:     df_after = df_full[df_full["date"] > inference_date].sort_values("date")
  349: 
  350:     result = {}
  351:     for h in HORIZONS:
  352:         if h <= len(df_after):
  353:             target_date = pd.Timestamp(df_after["date"].iloc[h - 1])
  354:             actual_price = float(df_after["close"].iloc[h - 1])
  355:             actual_return = np.log(actual_price / base_price)
  356:             result[h] = {"date": target_date, "actual_price": actual_price, "actual_return": actual_return}
  357:         else:
  358:             # Estimate: H trading days â‰ˆ H * 7/5 calendar days
  359:             cal_days = int(h * 7 / 5) + 1
  360:             est_date = inference_date + pd.Timedelta(days=cal_days)
  361:             while est_date.dayofweek >= 5:
  362:                 est_date += pd.Timedelta(days=1)
  363:             result[h] = {"date": est_date, "actual_price": None, "actual_return": None}
  364: 
  365:     return result
  366: 
  367: 
  368: def compute_regime_shift_score(X: np.ndarray, recent: int = 60, reference: int = 252) -> float:
  369:     """Causal standardized mean shift: recent window vs prior reference window."""
  370:     if len(X) < recent + reference:
  371:         return 0.0
  372:     ref, cur = X[-recent-reference:-recent], X[-recent:]
  373:     scale = np.nanstd(ref, axis=0)
  374:     scale[scale < 1e-12] = 1.0
  375:     distance = np.abs(np.nanmean(cur, axis=0) - np.nanmean(ref, axis=0)) / scale
  376:     return float(np.nanmean(distance))
  377: 
  378: 
  379: def compute_direction_regime(close: pd.Series, lookback: int = 20, reference: int = 100) -> Tuple[str, float, int]:
  380:     """Causal directional shift with neutral hysteresis context."""
  381:     ret = np.log(close / close.shift(1)).dropna()
  382:     if len(ret) < lookback + reference:
  383:         return "transition", 0.0, 0
  384:     recent, ref = ret.iloc[-lookback:], ret.iloc[-lookback-reference:-lookback]
  385:     p_recent, p_ref = float((recent < 0).mean()), float((ref < 0).mean())
  386:     se = np.sqrt(max(p_ref * (1 - p_ref) / len(recent), 1e-6))
  387:     z = (p_recent - p_ref) / se
  388:     mr, mf = float(recent.mean()), float(ref.mean())
  389:     if z > 1.5 and mr < mf:
  390:         state = "risk_off"

codex
La purga H ya tiene la forma correcta para labels \(y_t=\log(P_{t+H}/P_t)\): el último train admitido madura antes del primer test. El scaler del walk-forward sí se ajusta por fold. Estoy separando eso de otro problema distinto: el DA y los retornos concatenan decisiones diarias con ventanas H solapadas, así que no representan muestras independientes.

    1: """Causal weekly directional replay for USD/COP.
    2: 
    3: This module turns the research-grade PIT macro classifier into an immutable
    4: weekly publication contract.  It deliberately keeps model selection, feature
    5: selection, label maturity and horizon selection separate:
    6: 
    7: * features are selected with labels that mature before the configured cutoff;
    8: * model memory/thresholds are selected only in the configured validation years;
    9: * 2025 is inferred with models frozen at the end of 2024;
   10: * 2026 is refit at every weekly origin with only labels mature at that origin;
   11: * a horizon can enter the weekly consensus only from already-mature forecasts.
   12: 
   13: The resulting direction is a shadow/research decision.  Execution authorization
   14: is intentionally outside this contract.
   15: """
   16: from __future__ import annotations
   17: 
   18: from dataclasses import dataclass
   19: from datetime import datetime, timezone
   20: import hashlib
   21: import json
   22: from pathlib import Path
   23: from typing import Any, Iterable
   24: 
   25: import numpy as np
   26: import pandas as pd
   27: import yaml
   28: from sklearn.feature_selection import mutual_info_classif
   29: from sklearn.impute import SimpleImputer
   30: from sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression, Ridge
   31: from sklearn.pipeline import Pipeline, make_pipeline
   32: from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler
   33: 
   34: from scripts.analysis.weekly_forecasting_oos_canonical import build_frame
   35: 
   36: 
   37: SCHEMA_VERSION = "1.1.0"
   38: MODEL_FAMILY = "pit_macro_logistic"
   39: POINT_MODEL_FAMILY = "ridge_log_return"
   40: 
   41: 
   42: @dataclass(frozen=True)
   43: class ReplayPaths:
   44:     dashboard_dir: Path
   45:     index_file: Path
   46:     ledger_file: Path
   47:     report_ledger: Path
   48:     report_summary: Path
   49:     report_workbook: Path
   50: 
   51: 
   52: def load_replay_config(path: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
   53:     """Load and minimally validate the replay configuration."""
   54:     with path.open(encoding="utf-8") as handle:
   55:         cfg = yaml.safe_load(handle)
   56:     required = ("_meta", "asset", "protocol", "horizons", "selection", "outputs")
   57:     missing = [key for key in required if key not in cfg]
   58:     if missing:
   59:         raise ValueError(f"Directional replay config missing sections: {missing}")
   60:     horizons = tuple(int(value) for value in cfg["horizons"]["values"])
   61:     if horizons != (1, 5, 10, 15, 20, 25, 30):
   62:         raise ValueError(f"Unexpected USD/COP horizon contract: {horizons}")
   63:     if cfg["_meta"].get("signal_authorized") is not False:
   64:         raise ValueError("Directional replay must remain signal_authorized=false")
   65:     return cfg
   66: 
   67: 
   68: def resolve_paths(root: Path, cfg: dict[str, Any]) -> ReplayPaths:
   69:     outputs = cfg["outputs"]
   70:     dashboard = root / outputs["dashboard_dir"]
   71:     return ReplayPaths(
   72:         dashboard_dir=dashboard,
   73:         index_file=dashboard / outputs["index_file"],
   74:         ledger_file=dashboard / outputs["ledger_file"],
   75:         report_ledger=root / outputs["report_ledger"],
   76:         report_summary=root / outputs["report_summary"],
   77:         report_workbook=root / outputs["report_workbook"],
   78:     )
   79: 
   80: 
   81: def _safe_float(value: Any, digits: int = 6) -> float | None:
   82:     if value is None:
   83:         return None
   84:     try:
   85:         number = float(value)
   86:     except (TypeError, ValueError):
   87:         return None
   88:     if not np.isfinite(number):
   89:         return None
   90:     return round(number, digits)
   91: 
   92: 
   93: def _sha256_file(path: Path) -> str | None:
   94:     if not path.exists():
   95:         return None
   96:     digest = hashlib.sha256()
   97:     with path.open("rb") as handle:
   98:         for block in iter(lambda: handle.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
   99:             digest.update(block)
  100:     return digest.hexdigest()
  101: 
  102: 
  103: def _make_target(frame: pd.DataFrame, horizon: int) -> pd.Series:
  104:     future_return = _make_return_target(frame, horizon)
  105:     return (future_return > 0).where(future_return.notna()).astype(float)
  106: 
  107: 
  108: def _make_return_target(frame: pd.DataFrame, horizon: int) -> pd.Series:
  109:     """Forward log return whose label matures exactly ``horizon`` sessions later."""
  110:     return np.log(frame["close"].shift(-horizon) / frame["close"])
  111: 
  112: 
  113: def _weekly_origins(frame: pd.DataFrame, start: str) -> pd.DataFrame:
  114:     dates = pd.to_datetime(frame["date"])
  115:     origins = frame.assign(iso_week=dates.dt.strftime("%G-W%V")).groupby("iso_week").tail(1)
  116:     return origins[origins["date"] >= pd.Timestamp(start)].copy()
  117: 
  118: 
  119: def _eligible_train_indices(
  120:     target: pd.Series,
  121:     origin_index: int,
  122:     horizon: int,
  123: ) -> np.ndarray:
  124:     # The last included label ends at ``origin_index``.  Nothing crossing the
  125:     # prediction origin is admitted.
  126:     indices = np.arange(0, max(0, origin_index - horizon + 1))
  127:     return indices[target.iloc[indices].notna().to_numpy()]
  128: 
  129: 
  130: def _sample_weights(indices: np.ndarray, half_life: int | None) -> np.ndarray | None:
  131:     if half_life is None or not len(indices):
  132:         return None
  133:     ages = indices[-1] - indices
  134:     return np.exp(-np.log(2.0) * ages / half_life)
  135: 
  136: 
  137: def _new_model(c_value: float, max_iter: int) -> Pipeline:
  138:     return make_pipeline(
  139:         SimpleImputer(strategy="median"),
  140:         StandardScaler(),
  141:         LogisticRegression(C=c_value, max_iter=max_iter, random_state=23),
  142:     )
  143: 
  144: 
  145: def _fit_model(
  146:     frame: pd.DataFrame,
  147:     target: pd.Series,
  148:     features: list[str],
  149:     train_indices: np.ndarray,
  150:     half_life: int | None,
  151:     c_value: float,
  152:     max_iter: int,
  153: ) -> Pipeline:
  154:     if len(train_indices) < 300:
  155:         raise ValueError(f"Insufficient mature labels: {len(train_indices)}")
  156:     model = _new_model(c_value, max_iter)
  157:     weights = _sample_weights(train_indices, half_life)
  158:     kwargs: dict[str, Any] = {}
  159:     if weights is not None:
  160:         kwargs["logisticregression__sample_weight"] = weights
  161:     model.fit(
  162:         frame.loc[train_indices, features],
  163:         target.iloc[train_indices].astype(int),
  164:         **kwargs,
  165:     )
  166:     return model
  167: 
  168: 
  169: def _new_point_model(alpha: float) -> Pipeline:
  170:     return make_pipeline(
  171:         SimpleImputer(strategy="median"),
  172:         StandardScaler(),
  173:         Ridge(alpha=alpha),
  174:     )
  175: 
  176: 
  177: def _fit_point_model(
  178:     frame: pd.DataFrame,
  179:     target: pd.Series,
  180:     features: list[str],
  181:     train_indices: np.ndarray,
  182:     half_life: int | None,
  183:     alpha: float,
  184:     clip_quantiles: tuple[float, float],
  185: ) -> tuple[Pipeline, tuple[float, float]]:
  186:     """Fit a causal log-return point model and causal anti-extrapolation bounds."""
  187:     if len(train_indices) < 300:
  188:         raise ValueError(f"Insufficient mature point labels: {len(train_indices)}")
  189:     model = _new_point_model(alpha)
  190:     weights = _sample_weights(train_indices, half_life)
  191:     kwargs: dict[str, Any] = {}
  192:     if weights is not None:
  193:         kwargs["ridge__sample_weight"] = weights
  194:     y_train = target.iloc[train_indices].astype(float)
  195:     model.fit(frame.loc[train_indices, features], y_train, **kwargs)
  196:     lower, upper = np.quantile(y_train.to_numpy(), clip_quantiles)
  197:     return model, (float(lower), float(upper))
  198: 
  199: 
  200: def _point_prediction_row(
  201:     *,
  202:     frame: pd.DataFrame,
  203:     target: pd.Series,
  204:     origin_index: int,
  205:     horizon: int,
  206:     model: Pipeline,
  207:     clip_bounds: tuple[float, float],
  208:     features: list[str],
  209:     train_indices: np.ndarray,
  210: ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  211:     origin = frame.loc[origin_index]
  212:     base_price = float(origin["close"])
  213:     raw_return = float(model.predict(frame.loc[[origin_index], features])[0])
  214:     forecast_return = float(np.clip(raw_return, clip_bounds[0], clip_bounds[1]))
  215:     forecast_price = float(base_price * np.exp(forecast_return))
  216:     actual_return = target.iloc[origin_index]
  217:     actual_return = None if pd.isna(actual_return) else float(actual_return)
  218:     actual_price = None if actual_return is None else float(base_price * np.exp(actual_return))
  219:     label_end = None
  220:     if len(train_indices):
  220:     if len(train_indices):
  221:         label_index = int(train_indices[-1]) + horizon
  222:         if label_index < len(frame):
  223:             label_end = pd.Timestamp(frame.loc[label_index, "date"])
  224:     return {
  225:         "iso_week": pd.Timestamp(origin["date"]).strftime("%G-W%V"),
  226:         "origin_date": pd.Timestamp(origin["date"]),
  227:         "horizon_days": horizon,
  228:         "forecast_log_return": forecast_return,
  229:         "forecast_return_pct": float((np.exp(forecast_return) - 1.0) * 100.0),
  230:         "forecast_price": forecast_price,
  231:         "forecast_price_change": forecast_price - base_price,
  232:         "point_forecast_direction": "UP" if forecast_return >= 0 else "DOWN",
  233:         "point_forecast_clipped": bool(not np.isclose(raw_return, forecast_return)),
  234:         "actual_log_return": actual_return,
  235:         "actual_price": actual_price,
  236:         "point_abs_error_price": (
  237:             None if actual_price is None else abs(forecast_price - actual_price)
  238:         ),
  239:         "point_abs_error_pct": (
  240:             None if actual_price is None
  241:             else abs(forecast_price - actual_price) / actual_price * 100.0
  242:         ),
  243:         "point_train_count": int(len(train_indices)),
  244:         "point_train_label_end": label_end,
  245:     }
  246: 
  247: 
  248: def walk_forward_point_forecasts(
  249:     frame: pd.DataFrame,
  250:     features: list[str],
  251:     horizon: int,
  252:     half_life: int | None,
  253:     origin_start: str,
  254:     origin_end: str,
  255:     alpha: float,
  256:     clip_quantiles: tuple[float, float],
  257: ) -> pd.DataFrame:
  258:     """Causal weekly point forecasts for validation/model selection."""
  259:     target = _make_return_target(frame, horizon)
  260:     origins = _weekly_origins(frame, origin_start)
  261:     origins = origins[origins["date"] <= pd.Timestamp(origin_end)]
  262:     rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
  263:     for origin_index in origins.index:
  264:         train = _eligible_train_indices(target, int(origin_index), horizon)
  265:         if len(train) < 300:
  266:             continue
  267:         model, bounds = _fit_point_model(
  268:             frame, target, features, train, half_life, alpha, clip_quantiles
  269:         )
  270:         rows.append(_point_prediction_row(
  271:             frame=frame,
  272:             target=target,
  273:             origin_index=int(origin_index),
  274:             horizon=horizon,
  275:             model=model,
  276:             clip_bounds=bounds,
  277:             features=features,
  278:             train_indices=train,
  279:         ))
  280:     return pd.DataFrame(rows)
  281: 
  282: 
  283: def frozen_year_point_forecasts(
  284:     frame: pd.DataFrame,
  285:     features: list[str],
  286:     horizon: int,
  287:     half_life: int | None,
  288:     year: int,
  289:     alpha: float,
  290:     clip_quantiles: tuple[float, float],
  291: ) -> pd.DataFrame:
  292:     """Fit the numeric return model once before the frozen OOS year."""
  293:     target = _make_return_target(frame, horizon)
  294:     dates = pd.to_datetime(frame["date"])
  295:     cutoff_index = int(np.searchsorted(dates, pd.Timestamp(f"{year}-01-01")))
  296:     train = np.arange(0, max(0, cutoff_index - horizon))
  297:     train = train[target.iloc[train].notna().to_numpy()]
  298:     model, bounds = _fit_point_model(
  299:         frame, target, features, train, half_life, alpha, clip_quantiles
  300:     )
  301:     origins = _weekly_origins(frame, f"{year}-01-01")
  302:     origins = origins[origins["date"] < pd.Timestamp(f"{year + 1}-01-01")]
  303:     return pd.DataFrame([
  304:         _point_prediction_row(
  305:             frame=frame,
  306:             target=target,
  307:             origin_index=int(origin_index),
  308:             horizon=horizon,
  309:             model=model,
  310:             clip_bounds=bounds,
  311:             features=features,
  312:             train_indices=train,
  313:         )
  314:         for origin_index in origins.index
  315:     ])
  316: 
  317: 
  318: def expanding_year_point_forecasts(
  319:     frame: pd.DataFrame,
  320:     features: list[str],
  321:     horizon: int,
  322:     half_life: int | None,
  323:     year: int,
  324:     alpha: float,
  325:     clip_quantiles: tuple[float, float],
  326: ) -> pd.DataFrame:
  327:     """Refit the numeric return model weekly with mature labels only."""
  328:     target = _make_return_target(frame, horizon)
  329:     origins = _weekly_origins(frame, f"{year}-01-01")
  330:     origins = origins[origins["date"] < pd.Timestamp(f"{year + 1}-01-01")]
  331:     rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
  332:     for origin_index in origins.index:
  333:         train = _eligible_train_indices(target, int(origin_index), horizon)
  334:         if len(train) < 300:
  335:             continue
  336:         model, bounds = _fit_point_model(
  337:             frame, target, features, train, half_life, alpha, clip_quantiles
  338:         )
  339:         rows.append(_point_prediction_row(
  340:             frame=frame,
  341:             target=target,
  342:             origin_index=int(origin_index),
  343:             horizon=horizon,
  344:             model=model,
  345:             clip_bounds=bounds,
  346:             features=features,
  347:             train_indices=train,
  348:         ))
  349:     return pd.DataFrame(rows)
  350: 
  351: 
  352: def select_frozen_features(
  353:     frame: pd.DataFrame,
  354:     candidates: list[str],
  355:     horizon: int,
  356:     cutoff: str,
  357:     feature_count: int,
  358: ) -> list[str]:
  359:     """Select features using labels whose target ends strictly before cutoff."""
  360:     target = _make_target(frame, horizon)
  361:     dates = pd.to_datetime(frame["date"])
  362:     cutoff_index = int(np.searchsorted(dates, pd.Timestamp(cutoff)))
  363:     eligible = np.arange(0, max(0, cutoff_index - horizon))
  364:     eligible = eligible[target.iloc[eligible].notna().to_numpy()]
  365:     usable = [name for name in candidates if frame.loc[eligible, name].notna().sum() >= 250]
  366:     if not usable:
  367:         raise ValueError(f"No eligible features for H{horizon} before {cutoff}")
  368:     values = SimpleImputer(strategy="median").fit_transform(frame.loc[eligible, usable])
  369:     scores = mutual_info_classif(
  370:         values,
  371:         target.iloc[eligible].astype(int).to_numpy(),
  372:         random_state=23,
  373:     )
  374:     order = np.argsort(scores)[::-1][: min(feature_count, len(usable))]
  375:     return [usable[index] for index in order]
  376: 
  377: 
  378: def _target_metadata(
  379:     frame: pd.DataFrame,
  380:     origin_index: int,
  381:     horizon: int,
  382:     target: pd.Series,
  383: ) -> tuple[pd.Timestamp, bool, int | None]:
  384:     target_index = origin_index + horizon
  385:     if target_index < len(frame):
  386:         target_date = pd.Timestamp(frame.loc[target_index, "date"])
  387:         actual = None if pd.isna(target.iloc[origin_index]) else int(target.iloc[origin_index])
  388:         return target_date, False, actual
  389:     target_date = pd.Timestamp(frame.loc[origin_index, "date"]) + pd.offsets.BDay(horizon)
  390:     return target_date, True, None
  391: 
  392: 
  393: def _prediction_row(
  394:     *,
  395:     frame: pd.DataFrame,
  396:     target: pd.Series,
  397:     origin_index: int,
  398:     horizon: int,
  399:     model: Pipeline,
  400:     features: list[str],
  401:     train_indices: np.ndarray,
  402:     half_life: int | None,
  403:     training_mode: str,
  404: ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  405:     origin = frame.loc[origin_index]
  406:     probability = float(model.predict_proba(frame.loc[[origin_index], features])[0, 1])
  407:     target_date, estimated, actual = _target_metadata(frame, origin_index, horizon, target)
  408:     label_end = None
  409:     if len(train_indices):
  410:         label_index = int(train_indices[-1]) + horizon
  411:         if label_index < len(frame):
  412:             label_end = pd.Timestamp(frame.loc[label_index, "date"])
  413:     return {
  414:         "iso_week": pd.Timestamp(origin["date"]).strftime("%G-W%V"),
  415:         "origin_date": pd.Timestamp(origin["date"]),
  416:         "base_price": float(origin["close"]),
  417:         "horizon_days": horizon,
  418:         "target_date": target_date,
  419:         "target_date_estimated": bool(estimated),
  420:         "probability_up": probability,
  421:         "actual": actual,
  422:         "train_count": int(len(train_indices)),
  423:         "train_label_end": label_end,
  424:         "half_life": "expanding" if half_life is None else str(half_life),
  425:         "training_mode": training_mode,
  426:     }
  427: 
  428: 
  429: def walk_forward_probabilities(
  430:     frame: pd.DataFrame,
  431:     features: list[str],
  432:     horizon: int,
  433:     half_life: int | None,
  434:     origin_start: str,
  435:     origin_end: str,
  436:     c_value: float,
  437:     max_iter: int,
  438: ) -> pd.DataFrame:
  439:     """Generate causal weekly probabilities, including unresolved final targets."""
  440:     target = _make_target(frame, horizon)
  441:     origins = _weekly_origins(frame, origin_start)
  442:     origins = origins[origins["date"] <= pd.Timestamp(origin_end)]
  443:     rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
  444:     for origin_index in origins.index:
  445:         train = _eligible_train_indices(target, int(origin_index), horizon)
  446:         if len(train) < 300:
  447:             continue
  448:         model = _fit_model(
  449:             frame, target, features, train, half_life, c_value, max_iter
  450:         )
  451:         rows.append(_prediction_row(
  452:             frame=frame,
  453:             target=target,
  454:             origin_index=int(origin_index),
  455:             horizon=horizon,
  456:             model=model,
  457:             features=features,
  458:             train_indices=train,
  459:             half_life=half_life,
  460:             training_mode="weekly_expanding_validation",
  460:             training_mode="weekly_expanding_validation",
  461:         ))
  462:     return pd.DataFrame(rows)
  463: 
  464: 
  465: def frozen_year_probabilities(
  466:     frame: pd.DataFrame,
  467:     features: list[str],
  468:     horizon: int,
  469:     half_life: int | None,
  470:     year: int,
  471:     c_value: float,
  472:     max_iter: int,
  473: ) -> pd.DataFrame:
  474:     """Fit once before ``year`` and infer every weekly origin without refitting."""
  475:     target = _make_target(frame, horizon)
  476:     dates = pd.to_datetime(frame["date"])
  477:     cutoff_index = int(np.searchsorted(dates, pd.Timestamp(f"{year}-01-01")))
  478:     train = np.arange(0, max(0, cutoff_index - horizon))
  479:     train = train[target.iloc[train].notna().to_numpy()]
  480:     model = _fit_model(frame, target, features, train, half_life, c_value, max_iter)
  481:     origins = _weekly_origins(frame, f"{year}-01-01")
  482:     origins = origins[origins["date"] < pd.Timestamp(f"{year + 1}-01-01")]
  483:     rows = [
  484:         _prediction_row(
  485:             frame=frame,
  486:             target=target,
  487:             origin_index=int(origin_index),
  488:             horizon=horizon,
  489:             model=model,
  490:             features=features,
  491:             train_indices=train,
  492:             half_life=half_life,
  493:             training_mode=f"frozen_pre_{year}",
  494:         )
  495:         for origin_index in origins.index
  496:     ]
  497:     return pd.DataFrame(rows)
  498: 
  499: 
  500: def expanding_year_probabilities(
  501:     frame: pd.DataFrame,
  502:     features: list[str],
  503:     horizon: int,
  504:     half_life: int | None,
  505:     year: int,
  506:     c_value: float,
  507:     max_iter: int,
  508: ) -> pd.DataFrame:
  509:     """Refit at each origin from labels mature at that exact origin."""
  510:     target = _make_target(frame, horizon)
  511:     origins = _weekly_origins(frame, f"{year}-01-01")
  512:     origins = origins[origins["date"] < pd.Timestamp(f"{year + 1}-01-01")]
  513:     rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
  514:     for origin_index in origins.index:
  515:         train = _eligible_train_indices(target, int(origin_index), horizon)
  516:         if len(train) < 300:
  517:             continue
  518:         model = _fit_model(frame, target, features, train, half_life, c_value, max_iter)
  519:         rows.append(_prediction_row(
  520:             frame=frame,
  521:             target=target,
  522:             origin_index=int(origin_index),
  523:             horizon=horizon,
  524:             model=model,
  525:             features=features,
  526:             train_indices=train,
  527:             half_life=half_life,
  528:             training_mode="weekly_expanding_matured_labels",
  529:         ))
  530:     return pd.DataFrame(rows)
  531: 
  532: 
  533: def directional_metrics(frame: pd.DataFrame, threshold: float) -> dict[str, Any]:
  534:     matured = frame[frame["actual"].notna()].copy()
  535:     if matured.empty:
  536:         return {
  537:             "n": 0, "directional_accuracy": None, "balanced_accuracy": None,
  538:             "up_recall": None, "down_recall": None, "minimum_class_recall": None,
  539:             "brier": None, "prediction_up_rate": None, "actual_up_rate": None,
  540:             "up_count": 0, "down_count": 0,
  541:         }
  542:     actual = matured["actual"].astype(int)
  543:     prediction = (matured["probability_up"] >= threshold).astype(int)
  544:     up = actual.eq(1)
  545:     down = actual.eq(0)
  546:     up_recall = float(prediction[up].mean()) if up.any() else None
  547:     down_recall = float((1 - prediction[down]).mean()) if down.any() else None
  548:     balanced = (
  549:         0.5 * (up_recall + down_recall)
  550:         if up_recall is not None and down_recall is not None else None
  551:     )
  552:     minimum = (
  553:         min(up_recall, down_recall)
  554:         if up_recall is not None and down_recall is not None else None
  555:     )
  556:     return {
  557:         "n": int(len(matured)),
  558:         "directional_accuracy": float((prediction == actual).mean()),
  559:         "balanced_accuracy": balanced,
  560:         "up_recall": up_recall,
  561:         "down_recall": down_recall,
  562:         "minimum_class_recall": minimum,
  563:         "brier": float(np.mean((matured["probability_up"] - actual) ** 2)),
  564:         "prediction_up_rate": float(prediction.mean()),
  565:         "actual_up_rate": float(actual.mean()),
  566:         "up_count": int(up.sum()),
  567:         "down_count": int(down.sum()),
  568:     }
  569: 
  570: 
  571: def point_forecast_metrics(frame: pd.DataFrame) -> dict[str, Any]:
  572:     """Evaluate numeric prices against the honest no-change/spot baseline."""
  573:     matured = frame[frame["actual_log_return"].notna()].copy()
  574:     if matured.empty:
  575:         return {
  576:             "n": 0,
  577:             "mae_log_return": None,
  578:             "rmse_log_return": None,
  579:             "naive_mae_log_return": None,
  580:             "naive_rmse_log_return": None,
  581:             "mae_skill_vs_spot": None,
  582:             "rmse_skill_vs_spot": None,
  583:             "mae_price": None,
  584:             "mape_price": None,
  585:             "directional_accuracy": None,
  586:             "balanced_accuracy": None,
  587:             "up_recall": None,
  588:             "down_recall": None,
  589:             "minimum_class_recall": None,
  590:             "bias_log_return": None,
  591:         }
  592:     actual = matured["actual_log_return"].astype(float).to_numpy()
  593:     forecast = matured["forecast_log_return"].astype(float).to_numpy()
  594:     error = forecast - actual
  595:     mae = float(np.mean(np.abs(error)))
  596:     rmse = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(np.square(error))))
  597:     naive_mae = float(np.mean(np.abs(actual)))
  598:     naive_rmse = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(np.square(actual))))
  599:     direction_frame = pd.DataFrame({
  600:         "probability_up": (forecast >= 0).astype(float),
  601:         "actual": (actual >= 0).astype(int),
  602:     })
  603:     direction = directional_metrics(direction_frame, 0.5)
  604:     actual_price = matured["actual_price"].astype(float).to_numpy()
  605:     forecast_price = matured["forecast_price"].astype(float).to_numpy()
  606:     return {
  607:         "n": int(len(matured)),
  608:         "mae_log_return": mae,
  609:         "rmse_log_return": rmse,
  610:         "naive_mae_log_return": naive_mae,
  611:         "naive_rmse_log_return": naive_rmse,
  612:         "mae_skill_vs_spot": None if naive_mae <= 0 else float(1.0 - mae / naive_mae),
  613:         "rmse_skill_vs_spot": None if naive_rmse <= 0 else float(1.0 - rmse / naive_rmse),
  614:         "mae_price": float(np.mean(np.abs(forecast_price - actual_price))),
  615:         "mape_price": float(np.mean(np.abs(forecast_price - actual_price) / actual_price)),
  616:         "directional_accuracy": direction["directional_accuracy"],
  617:         "balanced_accuracy": direction["balanced_accuracy"],
  618:         "up_recall": direction["up_recall"],
  619:         "down_recall": direction["down_recall"],
  620:         "minimum_class_recall": direction["minimum_class_recall"],
  621:         "bias_log_return": float(np.mean(error)),
  622:     }
  623: 
  624: 
  625: def select_point_model_card(
  626:     variants: dict[float, pd.DataFrame],
  627:     point_cfg: dict[str, Any],
  628: ) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], pd.DataFrame]:
  629:     """Select Ridge alpha by validation MAE skill and calibrate an 80% interval."""
  630:     candidates: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
  631:     for alpha, predictions in variants.items():
  632:         metrics = point_forecast_metrics(predictions)
  633:         skill = metrics.get("mae_skill_vs_spot")
  634:         candidates.append({
  635:             "alpha": float(alpha),
  636:             "metrics": metrics,
  637:             "score": float(skill) if skill is not None and np.isfinite(skill) else -np.inf,
  638:         })
  639:     best = max(
  640:         candidates,
  641:         key=lambda item: (
  642:             item["score"],
  643:             -(item["metrics"].get("rmse_log_return") or np.inf),
  644:             -item["alpha"],
  645:         ),
  646:     )
  647:     selected = variants[best["alpha"]].copy()
  648:     matured = selected[selected["actual_log_return"].notna()]
  649:     residuals = np.abs(
  650:         matured["actual_log_return"].astype(float)
  651:         - matured["forecast_log_return"].astype(float)
  652:     )
  653:     interval_level = float(point_cfg["interval_level"])
  654:     residual_quantile = float(residuals.quantile(interval_level)) if len(residuals) else 0.0
  655:     metrics = dict(best["metrics"])
  656:     metrics["interval_coverage"] = (
  657:         float((residuals <= residual_quantile).mean()) if len(residuals) else None
  658:     )
  659:     minimum_skill = float(point_cfg["minimum_validation_mae_skill"])
  660:     eligible = bool(best["score"] >= minimum_skill)
  661:     return {
  662:         "model_family": POINT_MODEL_FAMILY,
  663:         "alpha": _safe_float(best["alpha"], 3),
  664:         "target": str(point_cfg["target"]),
  665:         "selection_metric": str(point_cfg["selection_metric"]),
  666:         "validation_eligible": eligible,
  667:         "validation_metrics": _json_metrics(metrics),
  668:         "interval_level": _safe_float(interval_level),
  669:         "interval_abs_log_return": _safe_float(residual_quantile),
  670:         "clip_quantiles": [float(value) for value in point_cfg["prediction_clip_quantiles"]],
  671:         "candidate_count": len(candidates),
  672:     }, selected
  673: 
  674: 
  675: def robust_score(metrics: dict[str, Any], weights: dict[str, float]) -> float:
  676:     values = (
  677:         metrics.get("directional_accuracy"),
  678:         metrics.get("balanced_accuracy"),
  679:         metrics.get("minimum_class_recall"),
  680:     )
  681:     if any(value is None or not np.isfinite(value) for value in values):
  682:         return float("-inf")
  683:     return float(
  684:         weights["directional_accuracy"] * values[0]
  685:         + weights["balanced_accuracy"] * values[1]
  686:         + weights["minimum_class_recall"] * values[2]
  687:     )
  688: 
  689: 
  690: def validation_candidate_is_eligible(metrics: dict[str, Any], selection: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
  691:     rate = metrics.get("prediction_up_rate")
  692:     recall = metrics.get("minimum_class_recall")
  693:     balanced = metrics.get("balanced_accuracy")
  694:     return bool(
  695:         balanced is not None
  696:         and balanced >= 0.50
  697:         and recall is not None
  698:         and recall >= float(selection["validation_minimum_class_recall"])
  699:         and rate is not None
  700:         and float(selection["validation_minimum_prediction_class_rate"])
  701:         <= rate
  702:         <= float(selection["validation_maximum_prediction_class_rate"])
  703:     )
  704: 
  705: 
  706: def select_model_card(
  707:     variants: dict[int | None, pd.DataFrame],
  708:     features: list[str],
  709:     horizon: int,
  710:     validation_years: Iterable[int],
  711:     thresholds: Iterable[float],
  712:     selection: dict[str, Any],
  713: ) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], pd.DataFrame]:
  714:     years = {int(year) for year in validation_years}
  715:     weights = selection["score_weights"]
  716:     candidates: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
  717:     for half_life, predictions in variants.items():
  718:         validation = predictions[predictions["origin_date"].dt.year.isin(years)]
  719:         for threshold in thresholds:
  720:             metrics = directional_metrics(validation, float(threshold))
  721:             candidates.append({
  722:                 "half_life_raw": half_life,
  723:                 "half_life": "expanding" if half_life is None else str(half_life),
  724:                 "threshold": float(threshold),
  725:                 "score": robust_score(metrics, weights),
  726:                 "eligible": validation_candidate_is_eligible(metrics, selection),
  727:                 "metrics": metrics,
  728:             })
  729:     eligible = [candidate for candidate in candidates if candidate["eligible"]]
  730:     pool = eligible or candidates
  731:     best = max(
  732:         pool,
  733:         key=lambda item: (
  734:             item["score"],
  735:             -(item["metrics"].get("brier") or 1.0),
  736:             -abs(item["threshold"] - 0.5),
  737:         ),
  738:     )
  739:     selected = variants[best["half_life_raw"]].copy()
  740:     selected["threshold"] = best["threshold"]
  741:     selected["prediction"] = (selected["probability_up"] >= best["threshold"]).astype(int)
  742:     feature_hash = hashlib.sha256("|".join(features).encode()).hexdigest()[:16]
  743:     card = {
  744:         "horizon_days": horizon,
  745:         "model_family": MODEL_FAMILY,
  746:         "half_life": best["half_life"],
  747:         "threshold": _safe_float(best["threshold"], 3),
  748:         "validation_years": sorted(years),
  749:         "validation_score": _safe_float(best["score"]),
  750:         "validation_eligible": bool(best["eligible"]),
  751:         "validation_metrics": _json_metrics(best["metrics"]),
  752:         "selected_features": features,
  753:         "feature_hash": feature_hash,
  754:         "candidate_count": len(candidates),
  755:     }
  756:     return card, selected
  757:

  759: def _json_metrics(metrics: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
  760:     return {
  761:         key: int(value) if key in {"n", "up_count", "down_count"} and value is not None
  762:         else _safe_float(value)
  763:         for key, value in metrics.items()
  764:     }

  766: 
  767: def horizon_evidence(
  768:     history: pd.DataFrame,
  769:     threshold: float,
  770:     origin_date: pd.Timestamp,
  771:     selection: dict[str, Any],
  772:     validation_eligible: bool,
  773: ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  774:     matured = history[
  775:         history["actual"].notna()
  776:         & (history["target_date"] <= origin_date)
  777:     ].sort_values("origin_date")
  778:     lookback = int(selection["evidence_lookback_weeks"])
  779:     matured = matured.tail(lookback)
  780:     metrics = directional_metrics(matured, threshold)
  781:     score = robust_score(metrics, selection["score_weights"])
  782:     n = int(metrics["n"])
  783:     shrinkage = int(selection["shrinkage_weeks"])
  784:     shrunk_score = (
  785:         0.5 + (score - 0.5) * n / (n + shrinkage)
  786:         if np.isfinite(score) else float("-inf")
  787:     )
  788:     rate = metrics.get("prediction_up_rate")
  789:     recall = metrics.get("minimum_class_recall")
  790:     balanced = metrics.get("balanced_accuracy")
  791:     eligible = bool(
  792:         validation_eligible
  793:         and n >= int(selection["minimum_matured_weeks"])
  794:         and metrics["up_count"] >= int(selection["minimum_class_observations"])
  795:         and metrics["down_count"] >= int(selection["minimum_class_observations"])
  796:         and balanced is not None
  797:         and balanced >= float(selection["minimum_balanced_accuracy"])
  798:         and recall is not None
  799:         and recall >= float(selection["minimum_class_recall"])
  800:         and rate is not None
  801:         and float(selection["minimum_prediction_class_rate"])
  802:         <= rate
  803:         <= float(selection["maximum_prediction_class_rate"])
  804:     )
  805:     return {
  806:         **_json_metrics(metrics),
  807:         "raw_score": _safe_float(score),
  808:         "shrunk_score": _safe_float(shrunk_score),
  809:         "eligible": eligible,
  810:     }
  811: 
  812: 
  813: def _price_regime(frame: pd.DataFrame, origin_date: pd.Timestamp) -> dict[str, Any]:
  814:     history = frame[frame["date"] <= origin_date].copy()
  815:     returns = np.log(history["close"] / history["close"].shift(1)).dropna()
  816:     if len(returns) < 120:
  817:         return {"state": "transition", "direction_shift_z": 0.0}
  818:     recent = returns.tail(20)
  819:     reference = returns.tail(120).head(100)
  820:     recent_down = float((recent < 0).mean())
  821:     reference_down = float((reference < 0).mean())
  822:     standard_error = np.sqrt(max(reference_down * (1 - reference_down) / len(recent), 1e-6))
  823:     z_value = (recent_down - reference_down) / standard_error
  824:     if z_value > 1.5 and float(recent.mean()) < float(reference.mean()):
  825:         state = "risk_off"
  826:     elif z_value < -1.5 and float(recent.mean()) > float(reference.mean()):
  827:         state = "risk_on"
  828:     else:
  829:         state = "transition"
  830:     return {"state": state, "direction_shift_z": _safe_float(z_value)}
  831: 
  832: 
  833: def _horizon_role(horizon: int, roles: dict[str, list[int]]) -> str:
  834:     for role, values in roles.items():
  835:         if horizon in [int(value) for value in values]:
  836:             return role
  837:     return "unassigned"
  838: 
  839: 
  840: def build_week_contract(
  841:     *,
  842:     frame: pd.DataFrame,
  843:     week_rows: pd.DataFrame,
  844:     histories: dict[int, pd.DataFrame],
  845:     model_cards: dict[int, dict[str, Any]],
  846:     cfg: dict[str, Any],
  847:     latest_week: str,
  848: ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  849:     origin_date = pd.Timestamp(week_rows["origin_date"].iloc[0])
  850:     iso_week = str(week_rows["iso_week"].iloc[0])
  851:     year = int(origin_date.year)
  852:     roles = cfg["horizons"]["roles"]
  853:     selection = cfg["selection"]
  854:     freeze_evidence = year == int(cfg["protocol"]["frozen_replay_year"])
  855:     evidence_date = pd.Timestamp(f"{year}-01-01") if freeze_evidence else origin_date
  856: 
  857:     horizon_outputs: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
  858:     for _, row in week_rows.sort_values("horizon_days").iterrows():
  859:         horizon = int(row["horizon_days"])
  860:         card = model_cards[horizon]
  861:         point_card = card["point_forecast"]
  862:         threshold = float(card["threshold"])
  863:         evidence = horizon_evidence(
  864:             histories[horizon], threshold, evidence_date, selection,
  865:             bool(card["validation_eligible"]),
  866:         )
  867:         prediction = "UP" if float(row["probability_up"]) >= threshold else "DOWN"
  868:         actual = None if pd.isna(row["actual"]) else int(row["actual"])
  869:         role = _horizon_role(horizon, roles)
  870:         direction_eligible = bool(evidence["eligible"] and role != "execution")
  871:         forecast_log_return = float(row["forecast_log_return"])
  872:         forecast_price = float(row["forecast_price"])
  873:         interval_width = float(point_card["interval_abs_log_return"])
  874:         interval_lower = float(row["base_price"] * np.exp(forecast_log_return - interval_width))
  875:         interval_upper = float(row["base_price"] * np.exp(forecast_log_return + interval_width))
  876:         point_direction = str(row["point_forecast_direction"])
  877:         actual_log_return = (
  878:             None if pd.isna(row["actual_log_return"]) else float(row["actual_log_return"])
  879:         )
  880:         actual_price = None if pd.isna(row["actual_price"]) else float(row["actual_price"])
  881:         horizon_outputs.append({
  882:             "horizon_days": horizon,
  883:             "role": role,
  884:             "target_date": pd.Timestamp(row["target_date"]).date().isoformat(),
  885:             "target_date_estimated": bool(row["target_date_estimated"]),
  886:             "probability_up": _safe_float(row["probability_up"]),
  887:             "threshold": _safe_float(threshold, 3),
  888:             "prediction": prediction,
  889:             "forecast_log_return": _safe_float(forecast_log_return),
  890:             "forecast_return_pct": _safe_float(row["forecast_return_pct"], 4),
  891:             "forecast_price": _safe_float(forecast_price, 2),
  892:             "forecast_price_change": _safe_float(row["forecast_price_change"], 2),
  893:             "forecast_interval_lower": _safe_float(interval_lower, 2),
  894:             "forecast_interval_upper": _safe_float(interval_upper, 2),
  895:             "forecast_interval_level": _safe_float(point_card["interval_level"]),
  896:             "point_forecast_direction": point_direction,
  897:             "direction_price_agree": bool(point_direction == prediction),
  898:             "point_forecast_clipped": bool(row["point_forecast_clipped"]),
  899:             "point_forecast_validation_eligible": bool(point_card["validation_eligible"]),
  900:             "actual": actual,
  901:             "actual_log_return": _safe_float(actual_log_return),
  902:             "actual_price": _safe_float(actual_price, 2),
  903:             "point_abs_error_price": _safe_float(row["point_abs_error_price"], 2),
  904:             "point_abs_error_pct": _safe_float(row["point_abs_error_pct"], 4),
  905:             "hit": None if actual is None else bool(actual == (1 if prediction == "UP" else 0)),
  906:             "train_count": int(row["train_count"]),
  907:             "train_label_end": (
  908:                 pd.Timestamp(row["train_label_end"]).date().isoformat()
  909:                 if pd.notna(row["train_label_end"]) else None
  910:             ),
  911:             "point_train_label_end": (
  912:                 pd.Timestamp(row["point_train_label_end"]).date().isoformat()
  913:                 if pd.notna(row["point_train_label_end"]) else None
  914:             ),
  915:             "training_mode": str(row["training_mode"]),
  916:             "model_family": MODEL_FAMILY,
  917:             "feature_hash": card["feature_hash"],
  918:             "validation_eligible": bool(card["validation_eligible"]),
  919:             "evidence": evidence,
  920:             "eligible_for_direction": direction_eligible,
  921:             "selected": False,
  922:             "selection_rank": None,
  923:         })
  924: 
  925:     ranked = sorted(
  926:         horizon_outputs,
  927:         key=lambda item: item["evidence"].get("shrunk_score")
  928:         if item["evidence"].get("shrunk_score") is not None else -1.0,
  929:         reverse=True,
  930:     )
  931:     for rank, item in enumerate(ranked, start=1):
  932:         item["selection_rank"] = rank
  933: 
  934:     selected: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
  935:     for sleeve in ("tactical", "swing"):
  936:         candidates = [
  937:             item for item in ranked
  938:             if item["role"] == sleeve
  939:         ]
  940:         if candidates:
  941:             candidates[0]["selected"] = True
  942:             selected.append(candidates[0])
  943: 
  944:     required = int(selection["required_confirming_sleeves"])
  945:     agreement = len(selected) >= required and len({item["prediction"] for item in selected}) == 1
  946:     promotion_gate_passed = bool(
  947:         agreement and all(item["eligible_for_direction"] for item in selected)
  948:     )
  949:     direction = selected[0]["prediction"] if agreement else "FLAT"
  950:     primary = max(
  951:         selected,
  952:         key=lambda item: item["evidence"].get("shrunk_score") or -1.0,
  953:         default=None,
  954:     )
  955:     confirmation = [item["horizon_days"] for item in selected if item is not primary]
  956:     margins = [
  957:         min(1.0, abs(float(item["probability_up"]) - float(item["threshold"])) / 0.20)
  958:         for item in selected
  959:     ]
  960:     confidence = float(np.mean(margins)) if agreement and margins else 0.0
  961:     action = {
  962:         "UP": "LONG_USD_SHORT_COP",
  963:         "DOWN": "SHORT_USD_LONG_COP",
  964:         "FLAT": "FLAT",
  965:     }[direction]
  966:     primary_actual = primary["actual"] if primary is not None else None
  967:     decision_hit = (
  968:         None if direction == "FLAT" or primary_actual is None
  969:         else bool(primary_actual == (1 if direction == "UP" else 0))
  970:     )
  971:     if not selected:
  972:         rationale = "No horizon candidate was available in both sleeves."
  973:     elif len(selected) < required:
  974:         rationale = "Only one horizon sleeve passed; confirmation is required."
  975:     elif not agreement:
  976:         rationale = "Tactical and swing horizons disagree; the policy abstains."
  977:     elif not promotion_gate_passed:
  978:         rationale = "Best causal candidates agree, but one or more promotion gates remain unmet."
  979:     else:
  980:         rationale = "Tactical and swing horizons passed causal gates and agree."
  981: 
  982:     latest_partial = iso_week == latest_week and origin_date.dayofweek < 4
  983:     image_name = cfg["outputs"]["image_template"].format(week=iso_week.replace("-", "_"))
  984:     return {
  985:         "iso_week": iso_week,
  986:         "year": year,
  987:         "origin_date": origin_date.date().isoformat(),
  988:         "origin_is_partial_week": bool(latest_partial),
  989:         "base_price": _safe_float(week_rows["base_price"].iloc[0], 2),
  990:         "training_mode": str(week_rows["training_mode"].iloc[0]),
  991:         "regime": _price_regime(frame, origin_date),
  992:         "decision": {
  993:             "direction": direction,
  994:             "action": action,
  995:             "status": "SHADOW" if direction != "FLAT" else "ABSTAIN",
  996:             "signal_authorized": False,
  997:             "promotion_gate_passed": promotion_gate_passed,
  998:             "primary_horizon": None if primary is None else primary["horizon_days"],
  999:             "confirmation_horizons": confirmation,
 1000:             "selected_horizons": [item["horizon_days"] for item in selected],
 1001:             "confidence_proxy": _safe_float(confidence),
 1002:             "target_date": None if primary is None else primary["target_date"],
 1003:             "forecast_price": None if primary is None else primary["forecast_price"],
 1004:             "forecast_return_pct": None if primary is None else primary["forecast_return_pct"],
 1005:             "forecast_interval_lower": (
 1006:                 None if primary is None else primary["forecast_interval_lower"]
 1007:             ),
 1008:             "forecast_interval_upper": (
 1009:                 None if primary is None else primary["forecast_interval_upper"]
 1010:             ),
 1011:             "actual": primary_actual,
 1012:             "hit": decision_hit,
 1013:             "rationale": rationale,
 1014:         },
 1015:         "horizons": sorted(horizon_outputs, key=lambda item: item["horizon_days"]),
 1016:         "image_path": f"usdcop/{image_name}",
 1017:     }
 1018: 
 1019: 
 1020: def _summary_for_records(records: list[dict[str, Any]], year: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
 1021:     weeks = [record for record in records if record["year"] == year]
 1022:     decisions = [record for record in weeks if record["decision"]["direction"] != "FLAT"]
 1023:     matured = [record for record in decisions if record["decision"]["actual"] is not None]
 1024:     decision_frame = pd.DataFrame([
 1025:         {
 1026:             "probability_up": 1.0 if record["decision"]["direction"] == "UP" else 0.0,
 1027:             "actual": record["decision"]["actual"],
 1028:         }
 1029:         for record in matured
 1030:     ])
 1031:     decision_metrics = directional_metrics(decision_frame, 0.5) if matured else directional_metrics(pd.DataFrame(columns=["probability_up", "actual"]), 0.5)
 1032:     horizon_metrics = []
 1033:     for horizon in (1, 5, 10, 15, 20, 25, 30):
 1034:         rows = []
 1035:         point_rows = []
 1036:         threshold = None
 1037:         for record in weeks:
 1038:             item = next(value for value in record["horizons"] if value["horizon_days"] == horizon)
 1039:             threshold = float(item["threshold"])
 1040:             rows.append({"probability_up": item["probability_up"], "actual": item["actual"]})
 1041:             point_rows.append({
 1042:                 "forecast_log_return": item["forecast_log_return"],
 1043:                 "actual_log_return": item["actual_log_return"],
 1044:                 "forecast_price": item["forecast_price"],
 1045:                 "actual_price": item["actual_price"],
 1045:                 "actual_price": item["actual_price"],
 1046:             })
 1047:         metrics = directional_metrics(pd.DataFrame(rows), float(threshold))
 1048:         point_metrics = point_forecast_metrics(pd.DataFrame(point_rows))
 1049:         horizon_metrics.append({
 1050:             "horizon_days": horizon,
 1051:             **_json_metrics(metrics),
 1052:             "point_forecast": _json_metrics(point_metrics),
 1053:         })
 1054:     return {
 1055:         "year": year,
 1056:         "weeks_total": len(weeks),
 1057:         "shadow_decisions": len(decisions),
 1058:         "abstentions": len(weeks) - len(decisions),
 1059:         "coverage": _safe_float(len(decisions) / len(weeks) if weeks else None),
 1060:         "matured_decisions": len(matured),
 1061:         "pending_decisions": len(decisions) - len(matured),
 1062:         "decision_metrics": _json_metrics(decision_metrics),
 1063:         "horizon_metrics": horizon_metrics,
 1064:     }
 1065:
