 1067: def build_directional_replay(root: Path, cfg: dict[str, Any]) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], pd.DataFrame]:
 1068:     """Build the complete 2025 frozen + 2026 expanding replay document."""
 1069:     protocol = cfg["protocol"]
 1070:     model_cfg = protocol["model"]
 1071:     point_cfg = protocol["point_forecast"]
 1072:     horizons = [int(value) for value in cfg["horizons"]["values"]]
 1073:     validation_years = [int(value) for value in protocol["validation_years"]]
 1074:     validation_start = f"{min(validation_years)}-01-01"
 1075:     validation_end = f"{max(validation_years)}-12-31"
 1076:     half_lives = [None if value is None else int(value) for value in protocol["half_lives_days"]]
 1077:     thresholds = [float(value) for value in protocol["probability_thresholds"]]
 1078:     point_alphas = [float(value) for value in point_cfg["alphas"]]
 1079:     clip_quantiles = tuple(float(value) for value in point_cfg["prediction_clip_quantiles"])
 1080:     if len(clip_quantiles) != 2 or not 0 <= clip_quantiles[0] < clip_quantiles[1] <= 1:
 1081:         raise ValueError(f"Invalid point forecast clip quantiles: {clip_quantiles}")
 1082: 
 1083:     frame, _, candidates = build_frame(include_forward_pit=True, promotion_only=False)
 1084:     frame = frame.sort_values("date").reset_index(drop=True)
 1085:     frame["date"] = pd.to_datetime(frame["date"])
 1086: 
 1087:     model_cards: dict[int, dict[str, Any]] = {}
 1088:     validation_history: dict[int, pd.DataFrame] = {}
 1089:     replay_by_horizon: dict[int, pd.DataFrame] = {}
 1090:     for horizon in horizons:
 1091:         features = select_frozen_features(
 1092:             frame, candidates, horizon,
 1093:             str(protocol["feature_selection_cutoff"]),
 1094:             int(protocol["feature_count"]),
 1095:         )
 1096:         variants = {
 1097:             half_life: walk_forward_probabilities(
 1098:                 frame, features, horizon, half_life,
 1099:                 validation_start, validation_end,
 1100:                 float(model_cfg["c"]), int(model_cfg["max_iter"]),
 1101:             )
 1102:             for half_life in half_lives
 1103:         }
 1104:         card, selected_validation = select_model_card(
 1105:             variants, features, horizon, validation_years, thresholds, cfg["selection"]
 1106:         )
 1107:         selected_half_life = None if card["half_life"] == "expanding" else int(card["half_life"])
 1108:         point_variants = {
 1109:             alpha: walk_forward_point_forecasts(
 1110:                 frame, features, horizon, selected_half_life,
 1111:                 validation_start, validation_end, alpha, clip_quantiles,
 1112:             )
 1113:             for alpha in point_alphas
 1114:         }
 1115:         point_card, _ = select_point_model_card(point_variants, point_cfg)
 1116:         selected_alpha = float(point_card["alpha"])
 1117:         frozen = frozen_year_probabilities(
 1118:             frame, features, horizon, selected_half_life,
 1119:             int(protocol["frozen_replay_year"]),
 1120:             float(model_cfg["c"]), int(model_cfg["max_iter"]),
 1121:         )
 1122:         expanding = expanding_year_probabilities(
 1123:             frame, features, horizon, selected_half_life,
 1124:             int(protocol["expanding_retrain_year"]),
 1125:             float(model_cfg["c"]), int(model_cfg["max_iter"]),
 1126:         )
 1127:         replay = pd.concat([frozen, expanding], ignore_index=True)
 1128:         point_frozen = frozen_year_point_forecasts(
 1129:             frame, features, horizon, selected_half_life,
 1130:             int(protocol["frozen_replay_year"]), selected_alpha, clip_quantiles,
 1131:         )
 1132:         point_expanding = expanding_year_point_forecasts(
 1133:             frame, features, horizon, selected_half_life,
 1134:             int(protocol["expanding_retrain_year"]), selected_alpha, clip_quantiles,
 1135:         )
 1136:         point_replay = pd.concat([point_frozen, point_expanding], ignore_index=True)
 1137:         point_columns = [
 1138:             column for column in point_replay.columns
 1139:             if column not in {"iso_week"}
 1140:         ]
 1141:         replay = replay.merge(
 1142:             point_replay[point_columns],
 1143:             on=["origin_date", "horizon_days"],
 1144:             how="left",
 1145:             validate="one_to_one",
 1146:         )
 1147:         replay["threshold"] = float(card["threshold"])
 1148:         replay["prediction"] = (replay["probability_up"] >= float(card["threshold"])).astype(int)
 1149:         card["point_forecast"] = point_card
 1150:         model_cards[horizon] = card
 1151:         validation_history[horizon] = selected_validation
 1152:         replay_by_horizon[horizon] = replay
 1153: 
 1154:     replay_frame = pd.concat(replay_by_horizon.values(), ignore_index=True)
 1155:     latest_week = str(replay_frame.sort_values("origin_date")["iso_week"].iloc[-1])
 1156:     histories = {
 1157:         horizon: pd.concat(
 1158:             [validation_history[horizon], replay_by_horizon[horizon]],
 1159:             ignore_index=True,
 1160:         ).sort_values("origin_date")
 1161:         for horizon in horizons
 1162:     }
 1163:     records = [
 1164:         build_week_contract(
 1165:             frame=frame,
 1166:             week_rows=week_rows,
 1167:             histories=histories,
 1168:             model_cards=model_cards,
 1169:             cfg=cfg,
 1170:             latest_week=latest_week,
 1171:         )
 1172:         for _, week_rows in replay_frame.groupby("iso_week", sort=True)
 1173:     ]
 1174: 
 1175:     config_path = root / "config/forecast_experiments/usdcop_directional_macro_replay_v1.yaml"
 1176:     lineage_paths = {
 1177:         "ohlcv": root / "seeds/latest/usdcop_daily_ohlcv.parquet",
 1178:         "classic_macro": root / "data/pipeline/04_cleaning/output/MACRO_DAILY_CLEAN.parquet",
 1179:         "forward_macro_pit": root / "data/pipeline/04_cleaning/output/USDCOP_FORWARD_MACRO_PIT.parquet",
 1180:     }
 1181:     contract_basis = {
 1182:         "config_sha256": _sha256_file(config_path),
 1183:         "models": model_cards,
 1184:         "data_cutoff": frame["date"].max().date().isoformat(),
 1185:     }
 1186:     contract_hash = hashlib.sha256(
 1187:         json.dumps(contract_basis, sort_keys=True, default=str).encode()
 1188:     ).hexdigest()[:16]
 1189:     replay_years = [int(value) for value in protocol["replay_years"]]
 1190:     document = {
 1191:         "schema_version": SCHEMA_VERSION,
 1192:         "contract_hash": contract_hash,
 1193:         "asset_id": cfg["asset"]["id"],
 1194:         "symbol": cfg["asset"]["symbol"],
 1195:         "chart_symbol": cfg["asset"]["chart_symbol"],
 1196:         "generated_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
 1197:         "data_cutoff": frame["date"].max().date().isoformat(),
 1198:         "latest_week": latest_week,
 1199:         "years": replay_years,
 1200:         "methodology": {
 1201:             "experiment_id": cfg["_meta"]["experiment_id"],
 1202:             "status": cfg["_meta"]["status"],
 1203:             "signal_authorized": False,
 1204:             "feature_selection_cutoff": str(protocol["feature_selection_cutoff"]),
 1205:             "validation_years": validation_years,
 1206:             "frozen_replay_year": int(protocol["frozen_replay_year"]),
 1207:             "expanding_retrain_year": int(protocol["expanding_retrain_year"]),
 1208:             "label_maturity_rule": str(protocol["label_maturity_rule"]),
 1209:             "horizon_selection": "matured_only_two_sleeve_consensus",
 1210:         },
 1211:         "lineage": {
 1212:             key: {"path": str(path.relative_to(root)).replace("\\", "/"), "sha256": _sha256_file(path)}
 1213:             for key, path in lineage_paths.items()
 1214:         },
 1215:         "models": {str(horizon): model_cards[horizon] for horizon in horizons},
 1216:         "summaries": [_summary_for_records(records, year) for year in replay_years],
 1217:         "weeks": records,
 1218:     }
 1219:     return document, replay_frame

 1291: def summary_frame(document: dict[str, Any]) -> pd.DataFrame:
 1292:     rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
 1293:     for summary in document["summaries"]:
 1294:         decision = summary["decision_metrics"]
 1295:         rows.append({
 1296:             "year": summary["year"],
 1297:             "scope": "selected_strategy",
 1298:             "horizon_days": None,
 1299:             "n_weeks": summary["weeks_total"],
 1300:             "n_matured": summary["matured_decisions"],
 1301:             "coverage": summary["coverage"],
 1302:             "directional_accuracy": decision["directional_accuracy"],
 1303:             "balanced_accuracy": decision["balanced_accuracy"],
 1304:             "up_recall": decision["up_recall"],
 1305:             "down_recall": decision["down_recall"],
 1306:             "brier": decision["brier"],
 1307:         })
 1308:         for metrics in summary["horizon_metrics"]:
 1309:             point = metrics.get("point_forecast", {})
 1310:             rows.append({
 1311:                 "year": summary["year"],
 1312:                 "scope": "horizon",
 1313:                 "horizon_days": metrics["horizon_days"],
 1314:                 "n_weeks": summary["weeks_total"],
 1315:                 "n_matured": metrics["n"],
 1316:                 "coverage": None,
 1317:                 "directional_accuracy": metrics["directional_accuracy"],
 1318:                 "balanced_accuracy": metrics["balanced_accuracy"],
 1319:                 "up_recall": metrics["up_recall"],
 1320:                 "down_recall": metrics["down_recall"],
 1321:                 "brier": metrics["brier"],
 1322:                 "point_mae_log_return": point.get("mae_log_return"),
 1323:                 "point_rmse_log_return": point.get("rmse_log_return"),
 1324:                 "point_mae_skill_vs_spot": point.get("mae_skill_vs_spot"),
 1325:                 "point_rmse_skill_vs_spot": point.get("rmse_skill_vs_spot"),
 1326:                 "point_mae_price": point.get("mae_price"),
 1327:                 "point_mape_price": point.get("mape_price"),
 1328:                 "point_directional_accuracy": point.get("directional_accuracy"),
 1329:                 "point_balanced_accuracy": point.get("balanced_accuracy"),
 1330:             })
 1331:     return pd.DataFrame(rows)

 1334: def validate_replay_document(
 1335:     document: dict[str, Any],
 1336:     root: Path,
 1337:     *,
 1338:     require_images: bool = False,
 1339: ) -> list[str]:
 1340:     errors: list[str] = []
 1341:     if document.get("schema_version") != SCHEMA_VERSION:
 1342:         errors.append("schema_version mismatch")
 1343:     if document.get("asset_id") != "usdcop":
 1344:         errors.append("asset_id must be usdcop")
 1345:     weeks = document.get("weeks") or []
 1346:     labels = [week.get("iso_week") for week in weeks]
 1347:     if len(labels) != len(set(labels)):
 1348:         errors.append("duplicate iso_week records")
 1349:     if labels != sorted(labels):
 1350:         errors.append("weeks are not sorted")
 1351:     expected_horizons = [1, 5, 10, 15, 20, 25, 30]
 1352:     for week in weeks:
 1353:         origin = pd.Timestamp(week["origin_date"])
 1354:         horizons = week.get("horizons") or []
 1355:         actual_horizons = [int(item["horizon_days"]) for item in horizons]
 1356:         if actual_horizons != expected_horizons:
 1357:             errors.append(f"{week['iso_week']}: invalid horizons {actual_horizons}")
 1358:         selected = [item for item in horizons if item.get("selected")]
 1359:         decision = week["decision"]
 1360:         if sorted(item["horizon_days"] for item in selected) != sorted(decision["selected_horizons"]):
 1361:             errors.append(f"{week['iso_week']}: selected horizon mismatch")
 1362:         if decision["direction"] != "FLAT":
 1363:             if len(selected) < 2 or len({item["prediction"] for item in selected}) != 1:
 1364:                 errors.append(f"{week['iso_week']}: directional decision lacks two-sleeve agreement")
 1365:         for item in horizons:
 1366:             label_end = item.get("train_label_end")
 1367:             point_label_end = item.get("point_train_label_end")
 1368:             if label_end and pd.Timestamp(label_end) > origin:
 1369:                 errors.append(f"{week['iso_week']} H{item['horizon_days']}: immature training label")
 1370:             if point_label_end and pd.Timestamp(point_label_end) > origin:
 1371:                 errors.append(f"{week['iso_week']} H{item['horizon_days']}: immature point label")
 1372:             if week["year"] == 2025 and label_end and pd.Timestamp(label_end) >= pd.Timestamp("2025-01-01"):
 1373:                 errors.append(f"{week['iso_week']} H{item['horizon_days']}: frozen replay used 2025 label")
 1374:             if (
 1375:                 week["year"] == 2025
 1376:                 and point_label_end
 1377:                 and pd.Timestamp(point_label_end) >= pd.Timestamp("2025-01-01")
 1378:             ):
 1379:                 errors.append(f"{week['iso_week']} H{item['horizon_days']}: point replay used 2025 label")
 1380:             if item["actual"] is None and item["hit"] is not None:
 1381:                 errors.append(f"{week['iso_week']} H{item['horizon_days']}: hit without actual")
 1382:             price = item.get("forecast_price")
 1383:             log_return = item.get("forecast_log_return")
 1384:             lower = item.get("forecast_interval_lower")
 1385:             upper = item.get("forecast_interval_upper")
 1386:             if any(value is None for value in (price, log_return, lower, upper)):
 1387:                 errors.append(f"{week['iso_week']} H{item['horizon_days']}: point forecast missing")
 1388:             else:
 1389:                 implied = float(week["base_price"]) * np.exp(float(log_return))
 1390:                 if abs(float(price) - implied) > 0.02:
 1391:                     errors.append(f"{week['iso_week']} H{item['horizon_days']}: inconsistent point price")
 1392:                 if not (0 < float(lower) <= float(price) <= float(upper)):
 1393:                     errors.append(f"{week['iso_week']} H{item['horizon_days']}: invalid price interval")
 1394:             if item["actual"] is None and (
 1395:                 item.get("actual_log_return") is not None or item.get("actual_price") is not None
 1396:             ):
 1397:                 errors.append(f"{week['iso_week']} H{item['horizon_days']}: unresolved point has actual")
 1398:         if require_images:
 1399:             image = root / "usdcop-trading-dashboard/public/forecasting" / week["image_path"]
 1400:             if not image.exists() or image.stat().st_size == 0:
 1401:                 errors.append(f"{week['iso_week']}: image missing")
 1402:     expected_years = set(document.get("years") or [])
 1403:     actual_years = {int(week["year"]) for week in weeks}
 1404:     if expected_years != actual_years:
 1405:         errors.append(f"year coverage mismatch: expected={expected_years}, actual={actual_years}")
 1406:     try:
 1407:         json.dumps(document, allow_nan=False)
 1408:     except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
 1409:         errors.append(f"document is not strict JSON: {exc}")
 1410:     return errors

    1: """Canonical weekly OOS directional audit for USD/COP.
    2: 
    3: One forecast origin per ISO week. Feature selection is frozen using pre-2025
    4: data. At every origin only labels matured by ``origin - horizon`` are used.
    5: Macro observations become available no earlier than T+1.
    6: """
    7: from __future__ import annotations
    8: 
    9: from pathlib import Path
   10: import argparse
   11: import sys
   12: 
   13: import numpy as np
   14: import pandas as pd
   15: from sklearn.feature_selection import mutual_info_classif
   16: from sklearn.impute import SimpleImputer
   17: from sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression
   18: from sklearn.metrics import balanced_accuracy_score
   19: from sklearn.pipeline import make_pipeline
   20: from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler
   21: 
   22: ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
   23: sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))
   24: 
   25: from src.forecasting.dataset_loader import ForecastingDatasetLoader
   26: from src.forecasting.ssot_config import ForecastingSSOTConfig
   27: 
   28: HORIZONS = (1, 5, 10, 15, 20, 25, 30)
   29: RAW_MACRO = [
   30:     "FXRT_INDEX_DXY_USA_D_DXY", "FXRT_SPOT_USDMXN_MEX_D_USDMXN",
   31:     "FXRT_SPOT_USDCLP_CHL_D_USDCLP", "VOLT_VIX_USA_D_VIX",
   32:     "CRSK_SPREAD_EMBI_COL_D_EMBI", "COMM_OIL_WTI_GLB_D_WTI",
   33:     "COMM_OIL_BRENT_GLB_D_BRENT", "COMM_METAL_GOLD_GLB_D_GOLD",
   34:     "COMM_AGRI_COFFEE_GLB_D_COFFEE", "FINC_BOND_YIELD10Y_USA_D_UST10Y",
   35:     "FINC_BOND_YIELD2Y_USA_D_DGS2", "FINC_RATE_IBR_OVERNIGHT_COL_D_IBR",
   36:     "POLR_POLICY_RATE_COL_M_TPM", "FINC_BOND_YIELD10Y_COL_D_COL10Y",
   37:     "FINC_BOND_YIELD5Y_COL_D_COL5Y", "EQTY_INDEX_COLCAP_COL_D_COLCAP",
   38: ]
   39: 
   40: 
   41: def build_frame(
   42:     *, include_forward_pit: bool = True, promotion_only: bool = False,
   43: ) -> tuple[pd.DataFrame, list[str], list[str]]:
   44:     cfg = ForecastingSSOTConfig.load()
   45:     df, _ = ForecastingDatasetLoader(cfg, project_root=ROOT).load_dataset()
   46:     df = df.sort_values("date").reset_index(drop=True)
   47:     macro = pd.read_parquet(
   48:         ROOT / "data/pipeline/04_cleaning/output/MACRO_DAILY_CLEAN.parquet"
   49:     ).reset_index()
   50:     macro = macro.rename(columns={macro.columns[0]: "date"})
   51:     # Conservative availability contract: observation dated T is usable T+1.
   52:     macro["date"] = pd.to_datetime(macro["date"]) + pd.Timedelta(days=1)
   53:     macro = macro[["date"] + RAW_MACRO].sort_values("date").drop_duplicates("date")
   54:     df = pd.merge_asof(df, macro, on="date", direction="backward")
   55: 
   56:     price_features = [
   57:         "return_1d", "return_5d", "return_10d", "return_20d",
   58:         "volatility_5d", "volatility_10d", "volatility_20d",
   59:         "rsi_14d", "ma_ratio_20d", "ma_ratio_50d",
   60:         "day_of_week", "month", "is_month_end",
   61:     ]
   62:     macro_features: list[str] = []
   63:     rate_cols = {c for c in RAW_MACRO if "RATE" in c or "YIELD" in c}
   64:     for col in RAW_MACRO:
   65:         df[col] = pd.to_numeric(df[col], errors="coerce").ffill()
   66:         for lag in (1, 5, 20):
   67:             name = f"{col}_{'diff' if col in rate_cols else 'ret'}_{lag}"
   68:             if col in rate_cols:
   69:                 df[name] = df[col].diff(lag)
   70:             else:
   71:                 df[name] = df[col].pct_change(lag)
   72:             macro_features.append(name)
   73:     # Economically interpretable carry/slope features.
   74:     df["col_curve_10y_5y"] = (
   75:         df["FINC_BOND_YIELD10Y_COL_D_COL10Y"] - df["FINC_BOND_YIELD5Y_COL_D_COL5Y"]
   76:     )
   77:     df["carry_ibr_us2y"] = (
   78:         df["FINC_RATE_IBR_OVERNIGHT_COL_D_IBR"] - df["FINC_BOND_YIELD2Y_USA_D_DGS2"]
   79:     )
   80:     df["carry_col10y_us10y"] = (
   81:         df["FINC_BOND_YIELD10Y_COL_D_COL10Y"]
   82:         - df["FINC_BOND_YIELD10Y_USA_D_UST10Y"]
   83:     )
   84:     df["carry_policy_us2y"] = (
   85:         df["POLR_POLICY_RATE_COL_M_TPM"] - df["FINC_BOND_YIELD2Y_USA_D_DGS2"]
   86:     )
   87:     macro_features += [
   88:         "col_curve_10y_5y", "carry_ibr_us2y", "carry_col10y_us10y",
   89:         "carry_policy_us2y",
   90:     ]
   91: 
   92:     # Relative/cross-asset features are more stationary than standalone levels.
   93:     for lag in (1, 5, 20):
   94:         mxn = f"FXRT_SPOT_USDMXN_MEX_D_USDMXN_ret_{lag}"
   95:         clp = f"FXRT_SPOT_USDCLP_CHL_D_USDCLP_ret_{lag}"
   96:         dxy = f"FXRT_INDEX_DXY_USA_D_DXY_ret_{lag}"
   97:         vix = f"VOLT_VIX_USA_D_VIX_ret_{lag}"
   98:         embi = f"CRSK_SPREAD_EMBI_COL_D_EMBI_ret_{lag}"
   99:         brent = f"COMM_OIL_BRENT_GLB_D_BRENT_ret_{lag}"
  100:         coffee = f"COMM_AGRI_COFFEE_GLB_D_COFFEE_ret_{lag}"
  101:         colcap = f"EQTY_INDEX_COLCAP_COL_D_COLCAP_ret_{lag}"
  102:         cop = f"return_{lag}d"
  103:         df[f"latam_fx_ret_{lag}"] = 0.5 * (df[mxn] + df[clp])
  104:         df[f"cop_latam_residual_{lag}"] = df[cop] - df[f"latam_fx_ret_{lag}"]
  105:         df[f"cop_dxy_residual_{lag}"] = df[cop] - df[dxy]
  106:         df[f"terms_of_trade_impulse_{lag}"] = df[brent] + df[coffee]
  107:         raw_risk = df[dxy] + df[vix] + df[embi] - df[brent] - df[colcap]
  108:         mean = raw_risk.rolling(252, min_periods=60).mean()
  109:         std = raw_risk.rolling(252, min_periods=60).std().replace(0, np.nan)
  110:         df[f"risk_off_impulse_z_{lag}"] = (raw_risk - mean) / std
  111:         macro_features += [
  112:             f"latam_fx_ret_{lag}", f"cop_latam_residual_{lag}",
  113:             f"cop_dxy_residual_{lag}", f"terms_of_trade_impulse_{lag}",
  114:             f"risk_off_impulse_z_{lag}",
  115:         ]
  116:     pit_features: list[str] = []
  117:     if include_forward_pit:
  118:         from src.data.usdcop_forward_macro import attach_forward_macro_features
  119: 
  120:         df, pit_features = attach_forward_macro_features(
  121:             df,
  122:             ROOT / "data/pipeline/04_cleaning/output/USDCOP_FORWARD_MACRO_PIT.parquet",
  123:             promotion_only=promotion_only,
  124:         )
  125:     df.attrs["forward_pit_features"] = pit_features
  126:     df.attrs["classic_macro_features"] = list(macro_features)
  127:     return df, price_features, price_features + macro_features + pit_features
  128: 
  129: 
  130: def select_features(df: pd.DataFrame, candidates: list[str], horizon: int, k: int = 12) -> list[str]:
  131:     close = df["close"]
  132:     future_return = np.log(close.shift(-horizon) / close)
  133:     target = (future_return > 0).where(future_return.notna()).astype(float)
  134:     cutoff = int(np.searchsorted(pd.to_datetime(df["date"]), pd.Timestamp("2025-01-01")))
  135:     mask = pd.Series(False, index=df.index)
  136:     mask.iloc[: max(0, cutoff - horizon + 1)] = True
  137:     mask &= target.notna()
  138:     usable = [c for c in candidates if df.loc[mask, c].notna().sum() >= 250]
  139:     imp = SimpleImputer(strategy="median")
  140:     x = imp.fit_transform(df.loc[mask, usable])
  141:     y = target.loc[mask].astype(int).to_numpy()
  142:     scores = mutual_info_classif(x, y, random_state=17)
  143:     return [usable[i] for i in np.argsort(scores)[::-1][: min(k, len(usable))]]
  144: 
  145: 
  146: def evaluate(df: pd.DataFrame, candidates: list[str], feature_set: str) -> pd.DataFrame:
  147:     dates = pd.to_datetime(df["date"])
  148:     origins = df.assign(iso_week=dates.dt.strftime("%G-W%V")).groupby("iso_week").tail(1)
  149:     origins = origins[(origins["date"] >= "2025-01-01") & (origins["date"] <= "2026-12-31")]
  150:     rows: list[dict] = []
  151:     for horizon in HORIZONS:
  152:         features = select_features(df, candidates, horizon)
  153:         future_return = np.log(df["close"].shift(-horizon) / df["close"])
  154:         y = (future_return > 0).where(future_return.notna()).astype(float)
  155:         for origin_idx, origin in origins.iterrows():
  156:             target_idx = origin_idx + horizon
  157:             if target_idx >= len(df) or pd.isna(y.iloc[origin_idx]):
  158:                 continue
  159:             # Purge every training label whose target reaches the origin.
  160:             train_idx = np.arange(0, max(0, origin_idx - horizon + 1))
  161:             train_idx = train_idx[y.iloc[train_idx].notna().to_numpy()]
  162:             if len(train_idx) < 300:
  163:                 continue
  164:             model = make_pipeline(
  165:                 SimpleImputer(strategy="median"), StandardScaler(),
  166:                 LogisticRegression(C=0.1, max_iter=2000),
  167:             )
  168:             model.fit(df.loc[train_idx, features], y.iloc[train_idx].astype(int))
  169:             pred = int(model.predict(df.loc[[origin_idx], features])[0])
  170:             actual = int(y.iloc[origin_idx])
  171:             majority = int(y.iloc[train_idx].mean() >= 0.5)
  172:             rows.append({
  173:                 "feature_set": feature_set, "week": origin["iso_week"],
  174:                 "origin_date": origin["date"], "target_date": df.loc[target_idx, "date"],
  175:                 "horizon": horizon, "prediction": pred, "actual": actual,
  176:                 "correct": int(pred == actual), "majority_correct": int(majority == actual),
  177:                 "selected_features": "|".join(features),
  178:             })
  179:     return pd.DataFrame(rows)
  180: 
  181: 
  182: def summarize(pred: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
  183:     x = pred.copy()
  184:     x["period"] = np.where(x["week"].str.startswith("2025"), "2025_OOS", "2026_FORWARD")
  185:     rows = []
  186:     for keys, g in x.groupby(["feature_set", "period", "horizon"]):
  187:         rows.append({
  188:             "feature_set": keys[0], "period": keys[1], "horizon": keys[2],
  189:             "n_weeks": len(g), "da": g["correct"].mean(),
  190:             "majority_da": g["majority_correct"].mean(),
  191:             "balanced_da": balanced_accuracy_score(g["actual"], g["prediction"]),
  192:             "actual_up_rate": g["actual"].mean(),
  193:         })
  194:     return pd.DataFrame(rows)
  195: 
  196: 
  197: def main() -> None:
  198:     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
  199:     parser.add_argument("--promotion-only", action="store_true")
  200:     parser.add_argument("--output-prefix", default="weekly_forecasting_oos_canonical")
  201:     args = parser.parse_args()
  202:     df, base, enriched = build_frame(promotion_only=args.promotion_only)
  203:     pit_features = df.attrs.get("forward_pit_features", [])
  204:     classic = [feature for feature in enriched if feature not in set(pit_features)]
  205:     evaluations = [
  206:         evaluate(df, base, "stationary_price"),
  207:         evaluate(df, classic, "stationary_price_plus_macro"),
  208:     ]
  209:     if pit_features:
  210:         suffix = "promotion_pit" if args.promotion_only else "all_pit"
  211:         evaluations.append(evaluate(df, enriched, f"stationary_price_plus_macro_{suffix}"))
  212:     predictions = pd.concat(evaluations, ignore_index=True)
  213:     summary = summarize(predictions)
  214:     report = ROOT / "reports"
  215:     report.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
  216:     predictions.to_csv(report / f"{args.output_prefix}_predictions.csv", index=False)
  217:     summary.to_csv(report / f"{args.output_prefix}_summary.csv", index=False)
  218:     print(summary.to_string(index=False))
  219: 
  220: 
  221: if __name__ == "__main__":
  222:     main()

1339:    for column in ("observation_date", "reference_date", "release_date"):

1365:        ON CONFLICT (series_id, observation_date, available_at)

    1: """Point-in-time ingestion for forward-looking Colombian USD/COP data.
    2: 
    3: The existing macro tables are wide and keep one row per observation date.  That
    4: shape cannot preserve multiple publication vintages, so this module writes a
    5: long-form PIT dataset whose primary time contract is ``available_at``.
    6: 
    7: Official sources supported here:
    8: 
    9: * Banco de la Republica (SUAMECA) daily USD/COP forward bulletins.
   10: * Banco de la Republica monthly derivatives reports.
   11: * Banco de la Republica EME analyst expectations workbooks.
   12: * Superfinanciera pension-fund derivative positions (historic Socrata format
   13:   468 and current portfolio workbook format 415).
   14: 
   15: Raw documents are cached under ``data/pipeline/01_sources`` and every output is
   16: hashed.  Historical rows with a reconstructed release lag are retained for
   17: research but explicitly marked as not promotion eligible.
   18: """

   20:

  115: def sha256_file(path: Path) -> str:
  116:     digest = hashlib.sha256()
  117:     with path.open("rb") as stream:
  118:         for chunk in iter(lambda: stream.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
  119:             digest.update(chunk)
  120:     return digest.hexdigest()
  121: 
  122: 
  123: def _ascii(value: Any) -> str:
  124:     text = "" if value is None else str(value)
  125:     text = text.replace("\u00a0", " ").replace("âˆ’", "-").replace("â€“", "-")
  126:     return "".join(
  127:         char for char in unicodedata.normalize("NFKD", text)
  128:         if not unicodedata.combining(char)
  129:     )
  130: 
  131: 
  132: def _compact(value: Any) -> str:
  133:     return re.sub(r"\s+", " ", _ascii(value)).strip()
  134: 
  135: 
  136: def _pdf_ascii(value: Any) -> str:
  137:     """Repair common glyph splits produced by LaTeX PDFs and pypdf."""
  138:     text = _ascii(value).replace("Ä±", "i")
  139:     repairs = {
  140:         r"\bF\s+echa\b": "Fecha",
  141:         r"\bT\s+otal\b": "Total",
  142:         r"\bdevaluaci\s+on\b": "devaluacion",
  143:         r"\bnegociaci\s+on\b": "negociacion",
  144:         r"\bparticipaci\s+on\b": "participacion",
  145:         r"\bposici\s+on\b": "posicion",
  146:         r"\bimpl\s+icita\b": "implicita",
  147:         r"\bte\s+orica\b": "teorica",
  148:         r"\bd\s+ias\b": "dias",
  149:         r"\bd\s+ia\b": "dia",
  150:         r"\bd\s+olar(?:es)?\b": "dolar",
  151:         r"\bn\s+umero\b": "numero",
  152:         r"\bGr\s+afico\b": "Grafico",
  153:         r"\bTama\s+no\b": "Tamano",
  154:         r"\ban\s+o\b": "ano",
  155:     }
  156:     for pattern, replacement in repairs.items():
  157:         text = re.sub(pattern, replacement, text, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
  158:     return text
  159: 
  160: 
  161: def parse_number(value: Any) -> float:
  162:     """Parse Spanish or English-formatted numbers without guessing silently."""
  163:     if value is None or (isinstance(value, float) and math.isnan(value)):
  164:         return math.nan
  165:     if isinstance(value, (int, float, np.number)):
  166:         return float(value)
  167:     text = _compact(value).replace("%", "").replace("US$", "").replace("USD", "")
  168:     text = re.sub(r"[^0-9,\.\-+]", "", text)
  169:     if not text or text in {"-", "+"}:
  170:         return math.nan
  171:     if "," in text:
  172:         # Official Colombian documents use dots for thousands and comma decimals.
  173:         text = text.replace(".", "").replace(",", ".")
  174:     elif text.count(".") > 1:
  175:         text = text.replace(".", "")
  176:     elif text.count(".") == 1:
  177:         left, right = text.split(".")
  178:         if len(right) == 3 and len(left.lstrip("-+")) <= 3:
  179:             text = left + right
  180:     try:
  181:         return float(text)
  182:     except ValueError:
  183:         return math.nan
  184: 
  185: 
  186: def parse_spanish_date(value: Any, default_year: int | None = None) -> pd.Timestamp | None:
  187:     text = _compact(value).lower()
  188:     text = re.sub(r"^el\s+", "", text)
  189:     text = text.replace("/", " ").replace(".", " ").replace("de ", "")
  190:     text = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", text).strip()
  191:     direct = pd.to_datetime(text, errors="coerce", dayfirst=True)
  192:     if not pd.isna(direct):
  193:         return pd.Timestamp(direct).normalize()
  194:     match = re.search(
  195:         r"(?P<day>\d{1,2})\s+(?P<month>[a-z]+)\s+(?P<year>\d{2,4})?", text
  196:     )
  197:     if not match:
  198:         return None
  199:     month = SPANISH_MONTHS.get(match.group("month").rstrip("."))
  200:     if not month:
  201:         return None
  202:     year_text = match.group("year")
  203:     year = int(year_text) if year_text else default_year
  204:     if year is None:
  205:         return None
  206:     if year < 100:
  207:         year += 2000 if year < 50 else 1900
  208:     try:
  209:         return pd.Timestamp(year=year, month=month, day=int(match.group("day")))
  210:     except ValueError:
  211:         # A malformed target date in one workbook must not abort the complete
  212:         # monthly history.  The caller can retain the value with a null target.
  213:         return None
  214: 
  215: 
  216: def _bogota_to_utc(value: datetime | pd.Timestamp, end_of_day: bool = False) -> pd.Timestamp:
  217:     stamp = pd.Timestamp(value)
  218:     if end_of_day and stamp.hour == stamp.minute == stamp.second == 0:
  219:         stamp = stamp + pd.Timedelta(hours=23, minutes=59, seconds=59)
  220:     if stamp.tzinfo is None:
  221:         stamp = stamp.tz_localize("America/Bogota")
  222:     return stamp.tz_convert("UTC")
  223: 
  224: 
  225: def _conservative_month_release(observation: pd.Timestamp, days: int = 31) -> pd.Timestamp:
  226:     return _bogota_to_utc(observation.normalize() + pd.Timedelta(days=days), end_of_day=True)
  227: 
  228: 
  229: def _valid_embedded_timestamp(
  230:     candidate: datetime | pd.Timestamp | None,
  231:     observation: pd.Timestamp,
  232:     max_lag_days: int,
  233: ) -> pd.Timestamp | None:
  234:     if candidate is None:
  235:         return None
  236:     stamp = _bogota_to_utc(candidate)
  237:     obs_utc = _bogota_to_utc(observation)
  238:     if obs_utc <= stamp <= obs_utc + pd.Timedelta(days=max_lag_days):
  239:         return stamp
  240:     return None
  241: 
  242: 
  243: def _pdf_text_and_created(path: Path) -> tuple[str, pd.Timestamp | None]:
  244:     try:
  245:         from pypdf import PdfReader
  246:     except ImportError as exc:  # pragma: no cover - dependency failure is explicit
  247:         raise RuntimeError("pypdf is required to parse official BanRep PDF reports") from exc
  248:     reader = PdfReader(str(path))
  249:     text = "\n".join(page.extract_text() or "" for page in reader.pages)
  250:     created: pd.Timestamp | None = None
  251:     metadata = reader.metadata
  252:     if metadata is not None:
  253:         try:
  254:             raw = metadata.creation_date or metadata.modification_date
  255:             if raw is not None:
  256:                 created = pd.Timestamp(raw)
  257:         except (AttributeError, ValueError, TypeError):
  258:             created = None
  259:     return text, created
  260: 
  261: 
  262: def _xlsx_created(path: Path) -> pd.Timestamp | None:
  263:     if path.suffix.lower() != ".xlsx":
  264:         return None
  265:     try:
  266:         with zipfile.ZipFile(path) as archive:
  267:             xml = archive.read("docProps/core.xml").decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
  268:         matches = re.findall(
  269:             r"<dcterms:(?:modified|created)[^>]*>([^<]+)</dcterms:(?:modified|created)>",
  270:             xml,
  271:         )
  272:         stamps = [pd.Timestamp(item) for item in matches]
  273:         return max(stamps) if stamps else None
  274:     except (KeyError, ValueError, zipfile.BadZipFile):
  275:         return None
  276: 
  277: 
  278: def _xls_created(path: Path) -> pd.Timestamp | None:
  279:     try:
  280:         import olefile
  281:     except ImportError:
  282:         return None
  283:     try:
  284:         with olefile.OleFileIO(str(path)) as ole:
  285:             stream = "\x05SummaryInformation"
  286:             if not ole.exists(stream):
  287:                 return None
  288:             props = ole.getproperties(stream)
  289:             candidates = [props.get(12), props.get(13)]  # created, last saved
  290:             stamps = [pd.Timestamp(item) for item in candidates if item is not None]
  291:             return max(stamps) if stamps else None
  292:     except (OSError, ValueError):
  293:         return None
  294: 
  295: 
  296: def parse_daily_forward_text(text: str) -> dict[str, float]:
  297:     """Extract stable features from a two-page BanRep daily forward report."""
  298:     normalized = _pdf_ascii(text)
  299:     flat = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", normalized)
  300:     result: dict[str, float] = {}
  301: 
  302:     total_match = re.search(
  303:         r"se pacto un total de US\$\s*([\d.,]+)\s*millones.*?"
  304:         r"devaluacion implicita.*?([\d.,]+)\s*%",
  305:         flat,
  306:         flags=re.IGNORECASE,
  307:     )
  308:     if total_match:
  309:         result["br_forward_daily_volume_usd_m"] = parse_number(total_match.group(1))
  310:         result["br_forward_daily_implied_devaluation_pct"] = parse_number(
  311:             total_match.group(2)
  312:         )
  313: 
  314:     table_one = normalized
  315:     if "Cuadro No. 1" in normalized and "Cuadro No. 2" in normalized:
  316:         table_one = normalized.split("Cuadro No. 1", 1)[1].split("Cuadro No. 2", 1)[0]
  317:     rows: dict[str, list[float]] = {}
  318:     for line in table_one.splitlines():
  319:         clean = _compact(line)
  320:         row_match = re.match(r"^(3-14|15-35|36-60|61-90|91-180|>180|Total)\s+(.+)$", clean)

 1387: 
 1388: def attach_forward_macro_features(
 1389:     market_frame: pd.DataFrame,
 1390:     pit_path: Path = DEFAULT_OUTPUT,
 1391:     *,
 1392:     decision_hour_bogota: int = 16,
 1393:     promotion_only: bool = False,
 1394:     max_age_days: Mapping[str, int] | None = None,
 1395: ) -> tuple[pd.DataFrame, list[str]]:
 1396:     """Attach strictly as-of forward-looking features to a daily market frame."""
 1397:     frame = market_frame.copy()
 1398:     if not Path(pit_path).exists():
 1399:         return frame, []
 1400:     pit = _coerce_pit_frame(pd.read_parquet(pit_path))
 1401:     if promotion_only:
 1402:         pit = pit[pit["promotion_eligible"]]
 1403:     if pit.empty:
 1404:         return frame, []
 1405:     local = pd.to_datetime(frame["date"]).dt.normalize() + pd.Timedelta(hours=decision_hour_bogota)
 1406:     decision = local.dt.tz_localize("America/Bogota", ambiguous="NaT", nonexistent="shift_forward").dt.tz_convert("UTC")
 1407:     left = pd.DataFrame({"decision_at": decision, "_row": np.arange(len(frame))}).sort_values("decision_at")
 1408:     age_limits = {"daily": 10, "monthly": 75}
 1409:     if max_age_days:
 1410:         age_limits.update({str(key): int(value) for key, value in max_age_days.items()})
 1411:     raw_columns: dict[str, str] = {}
 1412:     raw_values: dict[str, np.ndarray] = {}
 1413:     feature_names: list[str] = []
 1414:     for series_id, group in pit.groupby("series_id"):
 1415:         slug = re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", "_", series_id.lower()).strip("_")
 1416:         frequencies = group["frequency"].dropna().astype(str).str.lower()
 1417:         frequency = frequencies.iloc[-1] if not frequencies.empty else "monthly"
 1418:         group = group.sort_values(["available_at", "retrieved_at"]).drop_duplicates(
 1419:             "available_at", keep="last"
 1420:         )
 1421:         group = group[["available_at", "value"]].copy()
 1422:         group[f"{slug}__release_delta"] = group["value"].diff()
 1423:         denominator = group["value"].shift(1).abs().replace(0, np.nan)
 1424:         group[f"{slug}__release_pct"] = group["value"].diff() / denominator
 1425:         group = group.rename(columns={"available_at": "published_at", "value": slug})
 1426:         merged = pd.merge_asof(
 1427:             left,
 1428:             group.sort_values("published_at"),
 1429:             left_on="decision_at",
 1430:             right_on="published_at",
 1431:             direction="backward",
 1432:         ).sort_values("_row")
 1433:         age_days = (
 1434:             merged["decision_at"] - merged["published_at"]
 1435:         ).dt.total_seconds() / 86_400
 1436:         stale = age_days > age_limits.get(frequency, 75)
 1437:         value_columns = [slug, f"{slug}__release_delta", f"{slug}__release_pct"]
 1438:         merged.loc[stale, value_columns] = np.nan
 1439:         raw_values[slug] = merged[slug].to_numpy()
 1440:         raw_values[f"{slug}__release_delta"] = merged[f"{slug}__release_delta"].to_numpy()
 1441:         raw_values[f"{slug}__release_pct"] = merged[f"{slug}__release_pct"].to_numpy()
 1442:         raw_columns[series_id] = slug
 1443: 
 1444:     frame = pd.concat([frame, pd.DataFrame(raw_values, index=frame.index)], axis=1)
 1445:     engineered: dict[str, pd.Series] = {}
 1446: 
 1447:     def has(*series: str) -> bool:
 1448:         return all(item in raw_columns for item in series)
 1449: 
 1450:     def add(name: str, values: pd.Series) -> None:
 1451:         engineered[name] = values.replace([np.inf, -np.inf], np.nan)
 1452:         feature_names.append(name)
 1453: 
 1454:     near = raw_columns.get("br_eme_usdcop_near_mean")
 1455:     twelve = raw_columns.get("br_eme_usdcop_12m_mean")
 1456:     std = raw_columns.get("br_eme_usdcop_near_std")
 1457:     if near:
 1458:         add("pit_eme_near_gap_pct", frame[near] / frame["close"] - 1.0)
 1459:         add("pit_eme_near_revision_pct", frame[f"{near}__release_pct"])
 1460:     if near and twelve:
 1461:         add("pit_eme_curve_12m_pct", frame[twelve] / frame[near] - 1.0)
 1462:         add("pit_eme_12m_gap_pct", frame[twelve] / frame["close"] - 1.0)
 1463:         add("pit_eme_12m_revision_pct", frame[f"{twelve}__release_pct"])
 1464:     if near and std:
 1465:         add("pit_eme_near_dispersion", frame[std] / frame[near].abs().replace(0, np.nan))
 1466: 
 1467:     daily_volume = raw_columns.get("br_forward_daily_volume_usd_m")
 1468:     daily_dev = raw_columns.get("br_forward_daily_implied_devaluation_pct")
 1469:     if daily_volume:
 1470:         add("pit_forward_daily_volume_change", frame[f"{daily_volume}__release_pct"])
 1471:     if daily_dev:
 1472:         add("pit_forward_daily_devaluation", frame[daily_dev])
 1473:         add("pit_forward_daily_devaluation_change", frame[f"{daily_dev}__release_delta"])
 1474:         if "carry_policy_us2y" in frame:
 1475:             add("pit_forward_daily_devaluation_carry_gap", frame[daily_dev] - frame["carry_policy_us2y"])
 1476:     for source_id, name in (
 1477:         ("br_forward_daily_financial_net_usd_m", "financial"),
 1478:         ("br_forward_daily_offshore_net_usd_m", "offshore"),
 1479:         ("br_forward_daily_other_net_usd_m", "other"),
 1480:         ("br_forward_daily_ndf_3w_net_usd_m", "ndf_3w"),
 1481:     ):
 1482:         column = raw_columns.get(source_id)
 1483:         if column and daily_volume:
 1484:             add(f"pit_forward_daily_{name}_net_ratio", frame[column] / frame[daily_volume].abs().replace(0, np.nan))
 1485:     for source_id, name in (

.\docs\defense_qa.md:20:> R: El analisis tecnico clasico se basa en reglas fijas como "si el RSI cruza 30, comprar". Estas reglas son rigidas: no se adaptan a cambios de regimen del mercado, no ponderan multiples senales simultaneamente, y no aprenden de datos nuevos. El machine learning, en cambio, puede descubrir patrones complejos en 21 features simultaneas (indicadores tecnicos + variables macroeconomicas), adaptarse cada semana con reentrenamiento automatico, y cuantificar la incertidumbre de sus predicciones. En nuestro sistema, un modelo Ridge simple con 21 features logro +25.63% de retorno en 2025 (backtest fuera de muestra) contra -12.29% de buy-and-hold, con significancia estadistica p=0.006. Ademas, el ML permite automatizar el proceso completo: desde la ingestion de datos (7 fuentes, 39 DAGs de Airflow) hasta la ejecucion de operaciones, algo imposible con analisis tecnico manual. Sin embargo, es fundamental aclarar que en nuestro caso el alpha no proviene de la precision predictiva del modelo (que tiene R² negativo), sino del sistema completo que incluye un gate de regimen basado en el exponente de Hurst que sabe cuando NO operar.
.\docs\defense_qa.md:96:> R: Usamos 21 features organizados en tres categorias. Features tecnicos (del propio OHLCV): log_ret_5m (retorno logaritmico de corto plazo), rsi_9 (RSI de Wilder con periodo 9), atr_14 (Average True Range), adx_14 (Average Directional Index), y otros indicadores de momentum y volatilidad. Features macroeconomicos (Z-score normalizados): dxy_z (Dollar Index, impacto positivo en USD/COP), vix_z (volatilidad implicita del S&P 500, proxy de risk-off), wti_z (petroleo, impacto negativo porque Colombia es exportador), embi_z (spread soberano de Colombia, proxy de riesgo pais). Features adicionales en v2.0: vol_regime_ratio (ratio de volatilidad de corto/largo plazo) y trend_slope_60d (pendiente de tendencia a 60 dias). La seleccion se basa en la teoria economica del USD/COP: el tipo de cambio responde a diferenciales de tasas (IBR vs Fed Funds), precios de commodities (petroleo), flujos de capital (EMBI), y condiciones globales (DXY, VIX). Cada feature fue validado mediante walk-forward direction accuracy: solo se incluyen features que mejoran la DA out-of-sample.
.\docs\defense_qa.md:140:> R: Ridge (L2) penaliza la suma de los cuadrados de los coeficientes (||beta||²), mientras que Lasso (L1) penaliza la suma de los valores absolutos (||beta||_1). La diferencia practica es que Lasso puede forzar coeficientes exactamente a cero (seleccion de features), mientras que Ridge los encoge pero nunca los elimina. Para nuestro caso con 21 features, preferimos Ridge por tres razones: (1) no queremos eliminar features porque todas fueron seleccionadas con fundamento economico (DXY afecta USD/COP por canal de dolar global, WTI por exportaciones petroleras, VIX por risk-off). (2) Cuando features estan correlacionadas (e.g., DXY y EMBI tienden a comoverse), Lasso selecciona una arbitrariamente y elimina la otra, perdiendo informacion. Ridge distribuye el peso entre features correlacionadas. (3) Con solo ~260 observaciones semanales y 21 features, la regularizacion L2 proporciona estabilidad numerica sin sacrificar features potencialmente utiles. Bayesian Ridge va un paso mas alla al estimar la regularizacion automaticamente via inferencia bayesiana, eliminando la necesidad de cross-validation del hiperparametro alpha.
.\docs\defense_qa.md:186:> R: En nuestro contexto, el tradeoff bias-variance se manifiesta claramente en la comparacion entre Ridge (alto bias, baja varianza) y XGBoost (bajo bias, alta varianza). Ridge tiene un sesgo intrinseco porque asume una relacion lineal entre features y retornos. Si la relacion verdadera es no-lineal, Ridge la subestimara sistematicamente. Sin embargo, su varianza es baja: las predicciones no cambian drasticamente con pequenas perturbaciones de los datos. XGBoost puede capturar no-linealidades (bajo bias), pero sus predicciones varian significativamente con diferentes muestras de entrenamiento (alta varianza). En USD/COP, con ~260 puntos semanales y 21 features, estamos en un regimen de datos escasos donde la varianza domina el error total. Ridge acepta un poco mas de bias a cambio de mucha menos varianza, resultando en mejor desempeno out-of-sample. El colapso de varianza de los modelos de boosting es una manifestacion extrema de este tradeoff: los modelos reducen tanto su varianza que convergen a predicciones constantes (varianza = 0, bias maximo). La leccion es que para series de tiempo financieras con muestras pequenas, la regularizacion fuerte (alta bias, baja varianza) es preferible.
.\docs\defense_qa.md:214:> R: Esta es una omision parcialmente deliberada y parcialmente practica. ARIMA modela la estructura autocorrelativa de la serie temporal y es el benchmark clasico en econometria. GARCH modela la volatilidad condicional (heteroscedasticidad). No los incluimos como modelos de prediccion direccional por tres razones: (1) ARIMA sin features exogenas (ARIMAX) es univariado y no puede incorporar las 21 features macro/tecnicas que son el nucleo de nuestra propuesta. (2) GARCH predice volatilidad, no direccion; podria complementar nuestros stops adaptativos pero no reemplazar el modelo direccional. (3) La literatura reciente (Gu et al., 2020, "Empirical Asset Pricing via Machine Learning") muestra que modelos de ML superan sistematicamente a modelos econometricos clasicos para prediccion cross-section de retornos. Sin embargo, reconozco que incluir ARIMA/GARCH como baseline adicional habria fortalecido la tesis. En trabajo futuro, un modelo ARIMAX con las mismas features o un GARCH para la estimacion de volatilidad de los stops seria una extension natural.

    1: """
    2: Smart Simple v2.0 feature enhancement (SHARED SSOT).
    3: ====================================================
    4: 
    5: Single source of truth for the v2.0 feature enhancement used by BOTH the
    6: backtest/production export (`scripts/train_and_export_smart_simple.py`) AND the
    7: live weekly Airflow pipeline (H5-L3 training + H5-L5 signal).
    8: 
    9: Historically this logic lived only in the export script, so the live weekly
   10: pipeline trained/inferred on the 21 base features while the approved +25.63%
   11: backtest used 23 (base + vol_regime_ratio + trend_slope_60d). That divergence
   12: (audit A3-02) meant production models were not the approved models. Keeping the
   13: enhancement here â€” imported everywhere â€” guarantees train == backtest == infer.
   14: 
   15: The macro merge USED to be a silent no-op: it read `macro["fecha"]` (that is the
   16: DatetimeIndex, not a column) and looked for lowercase names that MACRO_DAILY_CLEAN
   17: does not use, so `carry_diff`/`term_spread` were filled with 0.0 and the enhanced
   18: set was effectively base (21) + vol_regime_ratio + trend_slope_60d = 23.
   19: 
   20: Fixed 2026-07-20: the merge now resolves the UPPERCASE SSOT columns and produces
   21: 25 features. `carry_diff` was renamed `rate_diff_ibr_ust2y` because it is a COP
   22: overnight vs US 2Y rate differential, not a pure FX carry (FedFunds is monthly and
   23: absent from the daily CLEAN) â€” naming it `carry_diff` overstated what it measures.
   24: 
   25: NOTE: this changes the feature set, so it is a NEW TRIAL under quant-constitution.md.
   26: Any edge claim must be re-established on the forward period, not on 2025.
   27: """
   28: import logging
   29: from pathlib import Path
   30: 
   31: import numpy as np
   32: import pandas as pd
   33: 
   34: logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
   35: 
   36: # Repo root = two levels up from this file (src/forecasting/enhance_v2.py).
   37: _DEFAULT_PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
   38: 
   39: 
   40: def enhance_features_v2(df, base_feature_cols, project_root=None, include_xlead=False):
   41:     """
   42:     Smart Simple v2.0 feature enhancement.
   43: 
   44:     Adds regime + carry + term spread features.
   45:     Transforms macro levels to 5d returns (stationary).
   46:     Keeps raw prices (Ridge needs them; XGBoost handles non-stationarity).
   47: 
   48:     Args:
   49:         df: DataFrame with at least `close`, `volatility_5d`, `volatility_20d`,
   50:             `date` columns.
   51:         base_feature_cols: iterable of the base feature column names.
   52:         project_root: repo root used to locate MACRO_DAILY_CLEAN.parquet. Defaults
   53:             to the repo root inferred from this file's location. The live DAGs
   54:             pass their own root (e.g. /opt/airflow).
   55: 
   56:     Returns: (df_enhanced, v2_feature_cols)
   57:     """
   58:     df = df.copy()
   59:     if project_root is None:
   60:         project_root = _DEFAULT_PROJECT_ROOT
   61: 
   62:     # --- New features ---
   63:     # Vol regime ratio (short vol / long vol)
   64:     vol5 = df["volatility_5d"].replace(0, np.nan)
   65:     vol20 = df["volatility_20d"].replace(0, np.nan)
   66:     df["vol_regime_ratio"] = (vol5 / vol20).clip(-5, 5).fillna(1.0)
   67: 
   68:     # Trend slope 60d (normalized)
   69:     def _trend_slope(series, window=60):
   70:         result = pd.Series(np.nan, index=series.index)
   71:         for i in range(window, len(series)):
   72:             chunk = series.iloc[i - window:i].values
   73:             if len(chunk) == window and np.std(chunk) > 0:
   74:                 x = np.arange(window)
   75:                 slope = np.polyfit(x, chunk, 1)[0]
   76:                 result.iloc[i] = slope / np.mean(chunk)
   77:         return result
   78: 
   79:     df["trend_slope_60d"] = _trend_slope(df["close"])
   80: 
  200:     method: "mean"             # Promedio simple de modelos
  201:     target: "ln(close[t+5]/close[t])"
  202: 
  203:   # Must match the live H5 signal adapter and forecasting_ssot (Ridge +
  204:   # BayesianRidge). XGBoost remains an offline experiment until separately
  205:   # promoted with its own immutable bundle.
  206:   use_xgboost: false

   68:     # Trend slope 60d (normalized)
   69:     def _trend_slope(series, window=60):
   70:         result = pd.Series(np.nan, index=series.index)
   71:         for i in range(window, len(series)):
   72:             chunk = series.iloc[i - window:i].values
   73:             if len(chunk) == window and np.std(chunk) > 0:
   74:                 x = np.arange(window)

   62: 
   63: def _atomic_csv(path: Path, frame) -> None:
   64:     path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   65:     temporary = path.with_suffix(path.suffix + ".tmp")
   66:     frame.to_csv(temporary, index=False)
   67:     os.replace(temporary, path)

   72:         "UP": "USD â†‘ / COP â†“",
   73:         "DOWN": "USD â†“ / COP â†‘",
   74:         "FLAT": "SIN POSICIÃ“N",

   78: def render_week_image(week: dict, output_path: Path) -> None:
   79:     """Render an as-of chart; no future realized label is drawn into the snapshot."""
   80:     horizons = week["horizons"]
   81:     labels = [f"H{item['horizon_days']}" for item in horizons]
   82:     x = np.arange(len(horizons))
   83:     probabilities = np.array([float(item["probability_up"]) for item in horizons])
   84:     thresholds = np.array([float(item["threshold"]) for item in horizons])
   85:     scores = np.array([
   86:         float(item["evidence"]["shrunk_score"])
   87:         if item["evidence"]["shrunk_score"] is not None else 0.5
   88:         for item in horizons
   89:     ])
   90:     evidence_n = [int(item["evidence"]["n"]) for item in horizons]
   91:     forecast_prices = np.array([float(item["forecast_price"]) for item in horizons])
   92:     interval_lower = np.array([float(item["forecast_interval_lower"]) for item in horizons])
   93:     interval_upper = np.array([float(item["forecast_interval_upper"]) for item in horizons])
   94:     base_price = float(week["base_price"])
   95: 
   96:     bar_colors = []
   97:     for item in horizons:
   98:         if item["selected"]:
   99:             bar_colors.append(COLORS["selected"])
  100:         elif item["role"] == "execution":
  101:             bar_colors.append(COLORS["execution"])
  102:         elif not item["eligible_for_direction"]:
  103:             bar_colors.append(COLORS["ineligible"])
  104:         else:
  105:             bar_colors.append(COLORS["up"] if item["prediction"] == "UP" else COLORS["down"])
  106: 
  107:     figure = plt.figure(figsize=(13.2, 10.0), facecolor=COLORS["background"])
  108:     grid = figure.add_gridspec(3, 1, height_ratios=[1.05, 1.12, 0.85], hspace=0.38)

  110:     price_ax = figure.add_subplot(grid[1])

  112:     for axis in (probability_ax, price_ax, evidence_ax):
  113:         axis.set_facecolor(COLORS["panel"])
  114:         axis.tick_params(colors=COLORS["muted"], labelsize=9)
  115:         axis.grid(axis="y", color=COLORS["grid"], alpha=0.55, linewidth=0.8)
  116:         for spine in axis.spines.values():
  117:             spine.set_color(COLORS["grid"])
  118: 
  119:     bars = probability_ax.bar(x, probabilities * 100, color=bar_colors, width=0.66, alpha=0.92)
  120:     probability_ax.scatter(
  121:         x, thresholds * 100, marker="D", s=38, color=COLORS["threshold"],
  122:         edgecolors=COLORS["background"], linewidths=0.7, zorder=4, label="Umbral fijado 2022â€“2024",
  123:     )
  124:     probability_ax.axhline(50, color=COLORS["muted"], linewidth=1, linestyle="--", alpha=0.55)

  126:     probability_ax.set_xticks(x, labels)
  127:     probability_ax.set_ylabel("Probabilidad USD/COP â†‘", color=COLORS["muted"], fontsize=10)
  128:     probability_ax.set_title(
  129:         "Inferencia por horizonte â€” amarillo = mejor candidato por sleeve Â· azul = timing H1",
  130:         loc="left", color=COLORS["text"], fontsize=11, fontweight="bold", pad=10,
  131:     )
  132:     probability_ax.legend(loc="upper right", frameon=False, labelcolor=COLORS["muted"], fontsize=8.5)
  133:     for bar, item, probability in zip(bars, horizons, probabilities):
  134:         marker = "âœ“" if item["selected"] else ""
  135:         probability_ax.text(
  136:             bar.get_x() + bar.get_width() / 2,
  137:             min(96, probability * 100 + 3.0),
  138:             f"{probability:.1%} {marker}",
  139:             ha="center", va="bottom", fontsize=9, color=COLORS["text"], fontweight="bold",
  140:         )

  143:             3.0,
  144:             item["prediction"],

  146:         )
  147: 
  148:     price_ax.plot(x, forecast_prices, color=COLORS["muted"], linewidth=1.2, alpha=0.65, zorder=2)
  149:     price_ax.errorbar(
  150:         x,
  151:         forecast_prices,
  152:         yerr=np.vstack([forecast_prices - interval_lower, interval_upper - forecast_prices]),
  153:         fmt="none",
  154:         ecolor=COLORS["grid"],

  161:         base_price, color=COLORS["threshold"], linewidth=1.2, linestyle="--",

  254: def validate_published(config_path: Path) -> None:
  255:     cfg = load_replay_config(config_path)
  256:     paths = resolve_paths(ROOT, cfg)
  257:     if not paths.index_file.exists():
  258:         raise FileNotFoundError(paths.index_file)
  259:     document = json.loads(paths.index_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
  260:     errors = validate_replay_document(document, ROOT, require_images=True)
  261:     if not paths.report_workbook.exists():
  262:         errors.append(f"workbook missing: {paths.report_workbook}")
  263:     if errors:
  264:         raise RuntimeError("Directional replay validation failed:\n- " + "\n- ".join(errors))
  265:     print(
  266:         f"[directional-replay] contract OK Â· {len(document['weeks'])} weeks Â· "
  267:         f"{document['weeks'][0]['iso_week']}..{document['weeks'][-1]['iso_week']} Â· "
  268:         f"hash={document['contract_hash']}"
  269:     )

  272: def main() -> None:
  273:     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
  274:     parser.add_argument("--config", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_CONFIG)
  275:     parser.add_argument("--skip-images", action="store_true")
  276:     parser.add_argument("--render-only", action="store_true")
  277:     parser.add_argument("--workbook-only", action="store_true")
  278:     parser.add_argument("--validate-only", action="store_true")
  279:     args = parser.parse_args()
  280: 
  281:     config_path = args.config if args.config.is_absolute() else ROOT / args.config
  282:     if args.validate_only:
  283:         validate_published(config_path)
  284:         return
  285: 
  286:     cfg = load_replay_config(config_path)
  287:     paths = resolve_paths(ROOT, cfg)
  288:     if args.workbook_only:
  289:         document = json.loads(paths.index_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
  290:         ledger = flatten_ledger(document)
  291:         summary = summary_frame(document)
  292:         export_directional_replay_workbook(paths.report_workbook, document, ledger, summary)
  293:         validate_published(config_path)
  294:         print(f"[directional-replay] workbook -> {paths.report_workbook.relative_to(ROOT)}")
  295:         return
  296:     if args.render_only:
  297:         document = json.loads(paths.index_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
  298:         for week in document["weeks"]:
  299:             output_path = ROOT / "usdcop-trading-dashboard/public/forecasting" / week["image_path"]
  300:             render_week_image(week, output_path)
  301:         validate_published(config_path)
  302:         print(f"[directional-replay] rendered {len(document['weeks'])} images")
  303:         return
  304:     print("[directional-replay] building frame, frozen models and weekly expanding replay...")
  305:     document, _ = build_directional_replay(ROOT, cfg)
  306:     initial_errors = validate_replay_document(document, ROOT, require_images=False)
  307:     if initial_errors:
  308:         raise RuntimeError("Pre-publication contract failed:\n- " + "\n- ".join(initial_errors))
  309: 
  310:     if not args.skip_images:
  311:         for index, week in enumerate(document["weeks"], start=1):
  312:             output_path = ROOT / "usdcop-trading-dashboard/public/forecasting" / week["image_path"]
  313:             render_week_image(week, output_path)
  314:             if index == 1 or index % 10 == 0 or index == len(document["weeks"]):
  315:                 print(f"[directional-replay] images {index}/{len(document['weeks'])}: {week['iso_week']}")
  316: 
  317:     ledger = flatten_ledger(document)
  318:     summary = summary_frame(document)
  319:     _atomic_json(paths.index_file, document)
  320:     _atomic_csv(paths.ledger_file, ledger)
  321:     _atomic_csv(paths.report_ledger, ledger)
  322:     _atomic_csv(paths.report_summary, summary)
  323:     export_directional_replay_workbook(paths.report_workbook, document, ledger, summary)
  324: 
  325:     errors = validate_replay_document(document, ROOT, require_images=not args.skip_images)
  326:     if errors:
  327:         raise RuntimeError("Published contract failed:\n- " + "\n- ".join(errors))
  328:     print(summary.to_string(index=False))
  329:     print(
  330:         f"[directional-replay] published {len(document['weeks'])} weeks, "
  331:         f"{len(ledger)} horizon rows -> {paths.index_file.relative_to(ROOT)}"
  332:     )
  333:

  336:     main()

  492: function DirectionalReplayPanel({ week, document }: {
  493:   week: DirectionalReplayWeek;
  494:   document: DirectionalReplayIndex;
  495: }) {
  496:   const decision = week.decision;
  497:   const selected = decision.selected_horizons.length
  498:     ? decision.selected_horizons.map((h) => `H${h}`).join(' + ')
  499:     : 'Ninguno';
  500:   const trainingLabel = week.year === document.methodology.frozen_replay_year
  501:     ? 'Congelado hasta 2024'
  502:     : 'Reentreno semanal causal';
  503:   const primaryForward = week.horizons.find((h) => h.horizon_days === decision.primary_horizon) ?? null;
  504:   const yearly = new Map(document.summaries.map((summary) => [summary.year, summary]));
  505:   const actionLabel = decision.direction === 'UP'
  506:     ? 'USD â†‘ / COP â†“'
  507:     : decision.direction === 'DOWN' ? 'USD â†“ / COP â†‘' : 'Sin posiciÃ³n';
  508: 
  509:   return (
  510:     <div className="flex flex-col gap-4" data-testid="forecasting-directional-replay">
  511:       <div className={`${GM.panelSoft} flex items-start gap-2.5 px-4 py-3`}>
  512:         <Info className={`w-4 h-4 shrink-0 mt-0.5 ${GM.accent}`} aria-hidden />
  513:         <p className={`m-0 ${GMT.meta} ${GM.textSec} leading-relaxed`}>
  514:           Replay causal por horizonte: features fijadas antes de 2022 y polÃ­tica calibrada en 2022â€“2024.

  911: // â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ vista principal

  913: export function ForecastingView() {
  914:   const router = useRouter();
  915:   const sp = useSearchParams();
  916:   const { data: session } = useSession();
  917:   const role = (session?.user as { role?: string } | undefined)?.role ?? 'free';
  918:   const isFree = role === 'free';
  919:   const isInternal = role === 'admin' || role === 'developer';
  920: 
  921:   // â”€â”€ estado en la URL (querystring) â”€â”€
  922:   const asset = resolveAnalysisAsset(sp.get('asset'));
  923:   const assetMeta = ANALYSIS_ASSETS.find((a) => a.asset_id === asset) ?? ANALYSIS_ASSETS[0];
  924:   // Model-zoo assets (USD/COP + BTC) share the 9-model ML surface; Gold = weekly inference.
  925:   const isModelZoo = assetMeta.forecast_mode === 'model_zoo';
  926:   // USD/COP keeps root paths (470 files unmoved); other zoo assets are namespaced by asset_id.
  927:   const csvPath = isModelZoo
  928:     ? (asset === 'usdcop'
  929:       ? '/api/forecasting/bi_dashboard_unified.csv'
  930:       : `/api/forecasting/${asset}/bi_dashboard_unified.csv`)
  931:     : null;
  932:   const pngBase = asset === 'usdcop' ? '/api/forecasting/' : `/api/forecasting/${asset}/`;
  933: 
  934:   const setParams = useCallback((patch: Record<string, string | null>) => {
  935:     const q = new URLSearchParams(sp.toString());
  936:     Object.entries(patch).forEach(([k, v]) => {
  937:       if (v === null || v === '') q.delete(k); else q.set(k, v);
  938:     });
  939:     const qs = q.toString();
  940:     router.replace(qs ? `/forecasting?${qs}` : '/forecasting', { scroll: false });
  941:   }, [router, sp]);
  942: 
  943:   // â”€â”€ datos (por activo; hooks incondicionales con path null) â”€â”€
  944:   const csv = useForecastCsv(csvPath);
  945:   const index = useGmQuery<WeeklyInferenceIndex>(!isModelZoo ? `/api/forecasting/${asset}/index.json` : null);
  946:   const forward = useGmQuery<ForwardDoc>(!isModelZoo ? `/api/forecasting/${asset}/forward.json` : null);
  947:   const directional = useGmQuery<DirectionalReplayIndex>(
  948:     asset === 'usdcop' ? '/api/forecasting/usdcop/directional_replay_index.json' : null,
  949:   );
  950: 
  951:   // â”€â”€ model zoo: derivar opciones del CSV â”€â”€
  952:   const rows = useMemo(() => csv.data ?? [], [csv.data]);
  953:   const zooWeeks = useMemo(
  954:     () => uniq(rows.filter((r) => r.view_type === 'forward_forecast'), 'inference_week'),
  955:     [rows],
  956:   );
  957:   const models = useMemo(() => uniq(rows, 'model_name'), [rows]);
  958:   const directionalWeeks = useMemo(
  959:     () => (directional.data?.weeks ?? []).map((record) => record.iso_week),
  960:     [directional.data],
  961:   );
  962: 
  963:   const modelParam = sp.get('model') ?? (asset === 'usdcop' ? DIRECTIONAL_REPLAY_MODEL : 'ALL');
  964:   const directionalSelected = asset === 'usdcop' && modelParam === DIRECTIONAL_REPLAY_MODEL;
  965:   const weeks = directionalSelected ? directionalWeeks : zooWeeks;
  966: 
  967:   const periodParam = sp.get('period');
  968:   const copView: ViewType = !directionalSelected && periodParam === 'backtest'
  969:     ? 'backtest' : 'forward_forecast';
  970:   const copWeek = copView === 'forward_forecast'
  971:     ? (periodParam && weeks.includes(periodParam) ? periodParam : weeks[weeks.length - 1] ?? '')
  972:     : '';
  973: 
  974:   const copModel = directionalSelected
  975:     ? DIRECTIONAL_REPLAY_MODEL
  976:     : modelParam !== 'ALL'
  977:     && (models.includes(modelParam) || ENSEMBLE_VARIANTS.some((v) => v.value === modelParam))
  978:     ? modelParam : 'ALL';
  979:   const copHorizons = useMemo(() => {
  980:     const hs = uniq(rows.filter((r) => r.view_type === copView), 'horizon_days');
  981:     return hs.sort((a, b) => Number(a) - Number(b));
  982:   }, [rows, copView]);
  983:   const hParam = sp.get('h') ?? 'ALL';
  984:   const copHorizon = hParam !== 'ALL' && copHorizons.includes(hParam) ? hParam : 'ALL';
  985:   const horizonEnabled = isModelZoo && !directionalSelected && copView === 'backtest' && copModel !== 'ALL';
  986: 
  987:   // â”€â”€ Oro/BTC: derivar opciones del index â”€â”€
  988:   const yearsAvail = useMemo(
  989:     () => [...(index.data?.years ?? [])].sort((a, b) => b - a),
  990:     [index.data],
 1080:         subtitle={directionalSelected
 1081:           ? `${assetMeta.display_name} Â· replay causal congelado 2025 + reentrenamiento semanal 2026`
 1082:           : isModelZoo
 1083:           ? `${assetMeta.display_name} Â· 9 modelos de Machine Learning (walk-forward) con consensus y ensembles`
 1084:           : `${assetMeta.display_name} Â· inferencia semanal basada en reglas: direcciÃ³n, exposiciÃ³n y rÃ©gimen para todo el aÃ±o`}
 1085:         actions={
 1086:           <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
 1087:             {isFree && <GmBadge tone="warn">T-1 semana Â· plan free</GmBadge>}
 1088:             <GmBadge tone="accent">{directionalSelected ? 'Causal Replay' : isModelZoo ? 'ML Model Zoo' : 'Weekly Inference'}</GmBadge>
 1089:           </span>
 1090:         }
 1091:       />
 1092: 
 1093:       {/* Filtros del prototipo: Activo Â· Periodo Â· Modelo Â· Horizonte (estado en URL) */}
 1094:       <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3.5 mb-5" data-testid="forecasting-filters">
 1095:         <GmDropdown
 1096:           label="Activos"
 1097:           value={asset}
 1098:           options={assetOptions}
 1099:           minW={220}
 1100:           onChange={(v) => setParams({ asset: v, period: null, model: null, h: null })}
 1101:         />
 1102:         <GmDropdown
 1103:           label="Periodo"
 1104:           value={isModelZoo ? (copView === 'backtest' ? 'backtest' : copWeek) : String(year ?? '')}
 1105:           options={periodOptions}
 1106:           minW={210}
 1107:           disabled={periodOptions.length === 0}
 1108:           onChange={(v) => setParams({ period: v, ...(v === 'backtest' ? {} : { h: null }) })}
 1109:         />
 1110:         <GmDropdown
 1111:           label={isModelZoo ? 'Modelo' : 'Estrategia'}
 1112:           value={isModelZoo ? copModel : strategyId}
 1113:           options={modelOptions}
 1114:           minW={200}
 1115:           mono={isModelZoo}
 1116:           disabled={modelOptions.length === 0}
 1117:           onChange={(v) => setParams({ model: v, h: null })}
 1118:         />
 1119:         <GmDropdown
 1120:           label="Horizonte"
 1121:           value={copHorizon}
 1122:           options={horizonOptions}
 1123:           minW={140}
 1124:           mono
 1125:           disabled={!horizonEnabled}
 1126:           onChange={(v) => setParams({ h: v === 'ALL' ? null : v })}
 1127:         />
 1128:       </div>
 1129: 
 1130:       {/* Cuerpo por activo */}
 1131:       {locked ? (

 1133:       ) : isModelZoo ? (

 1136:             state={directional}

    1: /**
    2:  * GET /api/forecasting/<path...> â€” serve a JSON/CSV file from public/forecasting with the PLAN
    3:  * gate (CTR-RBAC-001 R3). Middleware guarantees a session and stamps x-user-id; here we apply:
    4:  *   â€¢ asset scoping   â€” free â‡’ ['usdcop']; Gold/BTC weekly inference â‡’ 403 { upgrade:true }
    5:  *   â€¢ forecast delay  â€” free â‡’ 168h (T-1 week): current-week entries are stripped from
    6:  *                       weekly_inference_*.json AND rows newer than the cutoff are stripped from
    7:  *                       bi_dashboard_unified.csv (by `inference_date`), so free sees last week's
    8:  *                       forecast for their entitled asset too, paid sees live.
    9:  *
   10:  * Read-only, path-traversal-guarded. Legacy static /forecasting/<asset>/... still exists for
   11:  * build-time files but is asset-agnostic; the app fetches through here so the plan is enforced.
   12:  */
   13: import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
   14: import { promises as fs } from 'fs';
   15: import path from 'path';
   16: 
   17: import { getEntitlements } from '@/lib/auth/entitlements';
   18: 
   19: const FC_DIR = path.join(process.cwd(), 'public', 'forecasting');
   20: const KNOWN_ASSET = /^(usdcop|xauusd|btcusdt|spx500)$/;
   21: 
   22: /** Drop CSV rows whose `inference_date` is newer than (now - delayHours). Header preserved. */
   23: function applyCsvDelay(raw: string, delayHours: number): string {
   24:   if (!delayHours) return raw;
   25:   const cutoff = Date.now() - delayHours * 3_600_000;
   26:   const lines = raw.split(/\r?\n/);
   27:   if (lines.length < 2) return raw;
   28:   const header = lines[0].split(',');
   29:   const dateIdx = header.indexOf('inference_date');
   30:   if (dateIdx < 0) return raw;
   31:   const kept = [lines[0]];
   32:   for (let i = 1; i < lines.length; i++) {
   33:     if (!lines[i]) continue;
   34:     const t = Date.parse(lines[i].split(',')[dateIdx]);
   35:     if (Number.isNaN(t) || t <= cutoff) kept.push(lines[i]);
   36:   }
   37:   return kept.join('\n');
   38: }
   39: 
   40: /** Drop weekly-inference entries whose week is newer than (now - delayHours). */
   41: function applyDelay(json: unknown, delayHours: number): unknown {
   42:   if (!delayHours || typeof json !== 'object' || json === null) return json;
   43:   const cutoff = Date.now() - delayHours * 3_600_000;
   44:   const doc = json as { strategies?: Array<{ weeks?: Array<{ week_start?: string }> }> };
   45:   if (!Array.isArray(doc.strategies)) return json;
   46:   for (const s of doc.strategies) {
   47:     if (Array.isArray(s.weeks)) {
   48:       s.weeks = s.weeks.filter((w) => {
   49:         const t = w.week_start ? Date.parse(w.week_start) : NaN;
   50:         return Number.isNaN(t) || t <= cutoff;
   51:       });
   52:     }
   53:   }
   54:   return doc;
   55: }
   56: 
   57: /** Apply the same entitlement delay to the USD/COP directional replay ledger. */
   58: function applyDirectionalReplayDelay(json: unknown, delayHours: number): unknown {
   59:   if (!delayHours || typeof json !== 'object' || json === null) return json;
   60:   const cutoff = Date.now() - delayHours * 3_600_000;
   61:   const doc = json as {
   62:     latest_week?: string;
   63:     weeks?: Array<{ iso_week?: string; origin_date?: string }>;
   64:   };
   65:   if (!Array.isArray(doc.weeks)) return json;
   66:   doc.weeks = doc.weeks.filter((week) => {
   67:     const timestamp = week.origin_date ? Date.parse(week.origin_date) : NaN;
   68:     return Number.isNaN(timestamp) || timestamp <= cutoff;
   69:   });
   70:   doc.latest_week = doc.weeks.length ? doc.weeks[doc.weeks.length - 1]?.iso_week : undefined;
   71:   return doc;
   72: }
   73: 
   74: export async function GET(
   75:   req: Request,
   76:   { params }: { params: Promise<{ path: string[] }> },
   77: ) {
   78:   const { path: routePath } = await params;
   79:   const segments = routePath ?? [];
   80:   const leaf = segments.length ? segments[segments.length - 1] : '';
   81:   const isCsv = leaf.endsWith('.csv');
   82:   const isPng = leaf.endsWith('.png');
   83:   if (!segments.length || (!leaf.endsWith('.json') && !isCsv && !isPng)) {
   84:     return NextResponse.json({ error: 'only .json/.csv/.png files are served' }, { status: 400 });
   85:   }
   86:   if (segments.some((s) => s.includes('..') || s.includes('\0') || path.isAbsolute(s))) {
   87:     return NextResponse.json({ error: 'invalid path' }, { status: 400 });
   88:   }
   89: 
   90:   const ent = await getEntitlements(req.headers.get('x-user-id'));
   91:   // First segment is the asset when it matches a known id (â€¦/<asset>/weekly_inference_YYYY.json).
   92:   const asset = KNOWN_ASSET.test(segments[0]) ? segments[0] : 'usdcop';
   93:   if (!ent.assets.includes(asset)) {
   94:     return NextResponse.json(
   95:       { error: 'asset not in plan', asset, plan: ent.plan, upgrade: true },
   96:       { status: 403 },
   97:     );
   98:   }
   99: 
  100:   const target = path.resolve(FC_DIR, ...segments);
  101:   const rel = path.relative(FC_DIR, target);
  102:   if (rel.startsWith('..') || path.isAbsolute(rel)) {
  103:     return NextResponse.json({ error: 'path escapes forecasting dir' }, { status: 400 });
  104:   }
  105: 
  106:   try {
  107:     if (isPng) {
  108:       // Per-file PNG delay (R3 remainder): filenames carry the ISO week (`_YYYY_WNN`).
  109:       // A delayed plan must not fetch a chart newer than its cutoff even by direct URL.
  110:       if (ent.forecast_delay_hours) {
  111:         const m = leaf.match(/_(\d{4})_W(\d{2})/);
  112:         if (m) {
  113:           // Monday of that ISO week vs cutoff
  114:           const yr = Number(m[1]); const wk = Number(m[2]);
  115:           const jan4 = new Date(Date.UTC(yr, 0, 4));
  116:           const monday = new Date(jan4);
  117:           monday.setUTCDate(jan4.getUTCDate() - ((jan4.getUTCDay() + 6) % 7) + (wk - 1) * 7);
  118:           if (monday.getTime() > Date.now() - ent.forecast_delay_hours * 3_600_000) {
  119:             return NextResponse.json(
  120:               { error: 'chart delayed for your plan', upgrade: true }, { status: 403 });
  121:           }
  122:         }
  123:       }
  124:       const buf = await fs.readFile(target);
  125:       return new NextResponse(new Uint8Array(buf), {
  126:         headers: { 'Content-Type': 'image/png', 'Cache-Control': 'private, max-age=300' },
  127:       });
  128:     }
  129:     const raw = await fs.readFile(target, 'utf-8');
  130:     if (isCsv) {
  131:       const body = applyCsvDelay(raw, ent.forecast_delay_hours);
  132:       return new NextResponse(body, {
  133:         headers: { 'Content-Type': 'text/csv; charset=utf-8', 'Cache-Control': 'private, max-age=60' },
  134:       });
  135:     }
  136:     let doc = JSON.parse(raw);
  137:     if (/weekly_inference_\d+\.json$/.test(leaf)) {
  138:       doc = applyDelay(doc, ent.forecast_delay_hours);
  139:     } else if (leaf === 'directional_replay_index.json') {
  140:       doc = applyDirectionalReplayDelay(doc, ent.forecast_delay_hours);
  141:     }
  142:     return NextResponse.json(doc, { headers: { 'Cache-Control': 'private, max-age=60' } });
  143:   } catch (err) {
  144:     const code = (err as NodeJS.ErrnoException)?.code;
  145:     if (code === 'ENOENT') return NextResponse.json({ error: 'not found' }, { status: 404 });
  146:     return NextResponse.json({ error: 'failed to read file' }, { status: 500 });
  147:   }
  148: }
  220:   feature_hash: z.string(),
  221:   validation_eligible: z.boolean(),
  222:   evidence: DirectionalEvidenceSchema,
  223:   eligible_for_direction: z.boolean(),
  224:   selected: z.boolean(),
  225:   selection_rank: z.number().int().positive(),
  226: });

  236:   decision: z.object({
  237:     direction: z.enum(['UP', 'DOWN', 'FLAT']),
  238:     action: z.enum(['LONG_USD_SHORT_COP', 'SHORT_USD_LONG_COP', 'FLAT']),
  239:     status: z.enum(['SHADOW', 'ABSTAIN']),
  240:     signal_authorized: z.literal(false),
  241:     promotion_gate_passed: z.boolean(),
  242:     primary_horizon: z.number().int().positive().nullable(),
  243:     confirmation_horizons: z.array(z.number().int().positive()),
  244:     selected_horizons: z.array(z.number().int().positive()),
  245:     confidence_proxy: z.number().min(0).max(1),
  246:     target_date: z.string().nullable(),
  247:     forecast_price: z.number().positive().nullable(),
  248:     forecast_return_pct: z.number().nullable(),
  249:     forecast_interval_lower: z.number().positive().nullable(),
  250:     forecast_interval_upper: z.number().positive().nullable(),
  251:     actual: z.union([z.literal(0), z.literal(1)]).nullable(),
